1→"""Central command registry for CLI command handler resolution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→from functools import lru_cache
     8→
     9→CommandHandler = Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None]
    10→
    11→
    12→def _build_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    13→    """Import all command modules and build the handler dict on first access."""
    14→    from desloppify.app.commands.config import cmd_config
    15→    from desloppify.app.commands.detect import cmd_detect
    16→    from desloppify.app.commands.dev import cmd_dev
    17→    from desloppify.app.commands.exclude import cmd_exclude
    18→    from desloppify.app.commands.autofix.cmd import cmd_autofix
    19→    from desloppify.app.commands.langs import cmd_langs
    20→    from desloppify.app.commands.move.cmd import cmd_move
    21→    from desloppify.app.commands.next import cmd_next
    22→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd import cmd_plan
    23→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.suppress import cmd_suppress_pattern
    24→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.cmd import cmd_review
    25→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.cmd import cmd_scan
    26→    from desloppify.app.commands.show.cmd import cmd_show
    27→    from desloppify.app.commands.status import cmd_status
    28→    from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import cmd_update_skill
    29→    from desloppify.app.commands.viz import cmd_tree, cmd_viz
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.zone import cmd_zone
    31→
    32→    return {
    33→        "scan": cmd_scan,
    34→        "status": cmd_status,
    35→        "show": cmd_show,
    36→        "next": cmd_next,
    37→        "suppress": cmd_suppress_pattern,
    38→        "exclude": cmd_exclude,
    39→        "autofix": cmd_autofix,
    40→        "plan": cmd_plan,
    41→        "detect": cmd_detect,
    42→        "tree": cmd_tree,
    43→        "viz": cmd_viz,
    44→        "move": cmd_move,
    45→        "zone": cmd_zone,
    46→        "review": cmd_review,
    47→        "config": cmd_config,
    48→        "dev": cmd_dev,
    49→        "langs": cmd_langs,
    50→        "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
    51→    }
    52→
    53→
    54→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    55→def get_command_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    56→    """Return cached command handler dict, building on first access."""
    57→    return _build_handlers()
    58→
    59→
    60→__all__ = ["CommandHandler", "get_command_handlers"]
    61→

    22→    _add_suppress_parser,
    23→    _add_tree_parser,
    24→    _add_update_skill_parser,
    25→    _add_viz_parser,
    26→    _add_zone_parser,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser_groups_plan import add_plan_parser
    29→
    30→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    31→workflow:
    32→  scan       Run detectors, update state, show diff
    33→  status     Score dashboard with dimension health
    34→  next       Show next highest-priority item to work on
    35→  plan       Living plan: prioritize, cluster, resolve, skip, annotate
    36→
    37→investigate:
    38→  show       Dig into issues by file/dir/detector/ID
    39→  tree       Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    40→  viz        Interactive HTML treemap
    41→  detect     Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    42→
    43→improve:
    44→  autofix    Auto-fix mechanical issues
    45→  suppress   Suppress issues matching a pattern
    46→  exclude    Exclude path pattern from scanning
    47→  move       Move file/dir and update import references
    48→  review     Holistic subjective review (LLM-based)
    49→
    50→configure:
    51→  zone       Show/set zone classifications
    52→  config     Project configuration
    53→  langs      List language plugins
    54→  dev        Developer utilities
    55→  update-skill  Install/update agent skill document
    56→
    57→examples:
    58→  desloppify scan
    59→  desloppify scan --skip-slow --profile ci
    60→  desloppify plan                        # full prioritized markdown
    61→  desloppify plan queue                  # compact table of all items
    62→  desloppify next --count 10             # top 10 queue items
    63→  desloppify show src/components/Modal.tsx
    64→  desloppify plan resolve "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
    65→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
    66→  desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import
    67→"""
    68→
    69→
    70→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    71→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    72→
    73→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    74→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    75→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    76→
    77→
    78→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    79→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    80→    try:
    81→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    82→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    83→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    84→
    85→
    86→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    87→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    88→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    89→
    90→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    91→        prog="desloppify",
    92→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    93→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    94→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    95→    )
    96→    parser.add_argument(
    97→        "--lang",
    98→        type=str,
    99→        default=None,
   100→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   101→    )
   102→    parser.add_argument(
   103→        "--exclude",
   104→        action="append",
   105→        default=None,
   106→        metavar="PATTERN",
   107→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   108→    )
   109→    parser.add_argument(
   110→        "--version",
   111→        "-V",
   112→        action="version",
   113→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   114→    )
   115→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   116→        dest="command",
   117→        required=True,
   118→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   119→    )
   120→    # workflow
   121→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   124→    add_plan_parser(sub)
   125→    # investigate
   126→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   127→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   130→    # improve
   131→    _add_autofix_parser(sub, langs)
   132→    _add_suppress_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_exclude_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   135→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   136→    # configure
   137→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   138→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   139→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   140→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   141→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   142→    return parser
   143→
   144→
   145→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   146→
```

   368→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   369→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   370→    for lang_name in langs:
   371→        try:
   372→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   373→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   374→            fixer_names = []
   375→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   376→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   377→    return fixer_help_lines
   378→
   379→
   380→def _add_autofix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   381→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   382→        "autofix",
   383→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   384→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   385→    )
   386→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   387→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   388→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   389→    p_fix.add_argument(
   390→        "--dry-run",
   391→        action="store_true",

   393→    )

   400→    p_viz.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")

    52→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    53→    """Print the scan header line."""
    54→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    55→
    56→
    57→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    58→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    59→    lines = [
    60→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    61→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    62→    ]
    63→    print("\n".join(lines))
    64→
    65→
    66→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    67→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints.
    68→
    69→    Side-effect only: conditionally prints scan-mode warnings to stdout.
    70→    All branches may be skipped (full scan, no noise budget hit, no hidden
    71→    issues), so the function can legitimately produce no output.
    72→    """
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} issues hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
   101→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   102→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   103→        if impact:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   105→        if remediation:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _print_plan_workflow_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   110→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge_impl(state)
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   115→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   116→    try:
   117→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   118→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   119→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   120→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   121→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   122→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   123→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   124→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   125→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   126→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   127→        runtime,
   128→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   129→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   130→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   131→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   132→    )
   133→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   134→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   135→        print(
   136→            colorize(
   137→                "  * Subjective reset "
   138→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   139→                "yellow",
   140→            )
   141→        )
   142→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   143→        print(
   144→            colorize(
   145→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   146→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   147→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   148→                "yellow",
   149→            )
   150→        )
   151→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   152→
   153→    issues, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   154→    merge = orchestrator.merge(issues, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   155→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   156→
   157→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   158→
   159→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   160→
   161→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   162→    show_score_delta(
   163→        runtime.state,
   164→        merge.prev_overall,
   165→        merge.prev_objective,
   166→        merge.prev_strict,
   167→        merge.prev_verified,
   168→        target_strict=target_value,
   169→    )
   170→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   171→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   172→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   173→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   174→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   175→
   176→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   177→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   178→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   179→
   180→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   181→        merge.diff,
   182→        runtime.state,
   183→        runtime.lang,
   184→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   185→    )
   186→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   187→
   188→    write_query(
   189→        build_scan_query_payload(
   190→            runtime.state,
   191→            runtime.config,
   192→            runtime.profile,
   193→            merge.diff,
   194→            warnings,
   195→            narrative,
   196→            merge,
   197→            noise,
   198→        ),
   199→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    badge_path, _badge_result = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   203→    print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   204→    auto_update_skill()
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = [
   208→    "cmd_scan",
   209→]

    96→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    97→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})

    99→
   100→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   101→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   102→
   103→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
   104→    _plan = load_plan()
   105→    _plan_active = _plan if (
   106→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   107→    ) else None
   108→

   111→    narrative = compute_narrative(

   114→    )

   117→    # Build unified context once — plan, target_strict, and policy all agree.
   118→    _ctx = queue_context(

   123→    )

   134→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(

   170→        label = "never" if review_age == 0 else f"{review_age} days"
   171→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))
   172→    print()

   189→    )
   190→
   191→
   192→def _print_score_section(state, scores, plan, target_strict_score, ctx=None):
   193→    """Print score header: frozen plan-start or live score with queue breakdown."""
   194→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
   195→    breakdown = None
   196→    queue_remaining = 0
   197→    if plan_start_strict is not None:
   198→        try:
   199→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan, context=ctx)
   200→            queue_remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   201→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   202→            _logger.debug("Plan-aware queue count failed: %s", exc)
   203→            queue_remaining = 0
   204→    if plan_start_strict is not None and queue_remaining > 0:
   205→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   206→            plan, queue_remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   207→        )
   208→    else:
   209→        for line, style in score_summary_lines(
   210→            overall_score=scores.overall,
   211→            objective_score=scores.objective,
   212→            strict_score=scores.strict,
   213→            verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   214→            target_strict=target_strict_score,
   215→        ):
   216→            print(colorize(line, style))
   217→        # Show queue breakdown even without frozen score
   218→        if breakdown is None:
   219→            try:
   220→                breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan, context=ctx)
   221→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   222→                breakdown = None
   223→        if breakdown is not None and breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   224→            block = format_queue_block(breakdown)
   225→            for text, style in block:
   226→                print(colorize(text, style))
   227→    return breakdown
   228→
   229→
   230→def _status_json_payload(
   231→    state: dict,
   232→    stats: dict,
   233→    dim_scores: dict,
   234→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   235→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   236→    suppression: dict,
   237→) -> dict:
   238→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   239→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   240→    open_scope = (
   241→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(issues, state.get("scan_path"))
   242→        if isinstance(issues, dict)
   243→        else None
   244→    )
   245→    return {
   246→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   247→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   248→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   249→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   250→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   251→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   252→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   253→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   254→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   255→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   256→        "stats": stats,
   257→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   258→        "suppression": suppression,
   259→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   260→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   261→    }
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "cmd_status",
   265→    "show_dimension_table",
   266→    "show_focus_suggestion",
   267→    "show_ignore_summary",
   268→    "show_structural_areas",
   269→    "show_subjective_followup",
   270→]
   271→

    83→
    84→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    85→    state: dict,
    86→    dim_scores: dict,
    87→    *,

    89→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:

    91→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    92→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    93→        if entry.get("placeholder"):

    96→        if strict_val < threshold:
    97→            low.append(
    98→                (
    99→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
   100→                    strict_val,
   101→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   102→                )
   103→            )
   104→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
   105→    return low

   132→) -> str | None:

   136→        if active_cluster:
   137→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   138→    return effective_cluster

   154→    payload["objective_score"] = scores.objective
   155→    payload["strict_score"] = scores.strict

   157→        state,

   159→    )
   160→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   161→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   162→    ]
   163→    return payload

    33→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    34→    append_log_entry,
    35→    commit_tracking_summary,
    36→    load_plan,
    37→    purge_ids,
    38→    reset_plan,
    39→    save_plan,
    40→)
    41→
    42→
    43→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    45→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    46→    state = runtime.state
    47→
    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    52→    if config_warning:
    53→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    54→
    55→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    56→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    57→
    58→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    59→    if output:
    60→        try:
    61→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    62→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    63→            print_agent_plan(
    64→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    65→                next_command=next_command,
    66→            )
    67→        except OSError as e:
    68→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(plan_md)
    71→        print()
    72→        print_agent_plan(
    73→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    74→            next_command=next_command,
    75→        )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    79→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    80→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    84→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    85→    plan = load_plan()
    86→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    87→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    89→    kind_counts = {
    90→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    91→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    92→    }
    93→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    94→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    95→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    97→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    98→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    99→
   100→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   101→
   102→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   103→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   104→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   105→    if total_skipped:
   106→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   107→    else:
   108→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   109→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   110→    if clusters:
   111→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   112→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   113→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   115→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   116→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   117→    if described or noted:
   118→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   119→    if active:
   120→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   121→    if superseded:
   122→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   123→
   124→    # Commit tracking summary
   125→    _cfg = load_config()
   126→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   127→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   128→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   129→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   130→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   131→            print(
   132→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   133→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   138→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   142→    reset_plan(plan)
   143→    append_log_entry(
   144→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   145→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   146→    )
   147→    save_plan(plan)
   148→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   149→
   150→
   151→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   152→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   153→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   154→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   155→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   156→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   157→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   158→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   159→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   160→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   161→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   162→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   163→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   164→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   165→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   166→}
   167→
   168→
   169→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   170→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   171→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   172→    if plan_action is None:
   173→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   177→    if handler is None:
   178→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   179→        return
   180→    handler(args)
   181→
   182→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   183→

1→"""show command: dig into issues by file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_triage_guardrail_info
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan

    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    18→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan

    20→
    21→from .concerns_view import _show_concerns

    23→    _load_dimension_issues,
    24→    _render_clean_mechanical_dimension,
    25→    _render_no_matches,
    26→    _render_subjective_dimension,
    27→    _render_subjective_views_guide,
    28→)

    31→    render_issues,
    32→    show_agent_plan,

    36→from .scope import load_matches, resolve_entity, resolve_noise, resolve_show_scope
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    40→class ShowOptions:
    41→    """All user-facing show command options extracted once from argparse."""
    42→
    43→    pattern_raw: str = ""
    44→    show_code: bool = False
    45→    chronic: bool = False
    46→    no_budget: bool = False
    47→    output_file: str | None = None
    48→    top: int = 20
    49→
    50→    @classmethod
    51→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ShowOptions:
    52→        return cls(
    53→            pattern_raw=str(getattr(args, "pattern", "") or ""),
    54→            show_code=bool(getattr(args, "code", False)),
    55→            chronic=bool(getattr(args, "chronic", False)),
    56→            no_budget=bool(getattr(args, "no_budget", False)),
    57→            output_file=getattr(args, "output", None),
    58→            top=int(getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20),
    59→        )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _handle_special_entity_views(
    63→    *,
    64→    entity,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    config: dict,
    67→    lang_name: str | None,
    68→    pattern_raw: str,
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    71→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    72→        return True
    73→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    74→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    75→        return True
    76→    return False
    77→
    78→
    79→def _load_entity_matches(
    80→    *,
    81→    state: dict,
    82→    entity,
    83→    pattern: str,
    84→    status_filter: str,
    85→    scope: str | None,
    86→    chronic: bool,
    87→) -> tuple[str, list[dict]] | None:
    88→    if entity.kind != "dimension" or entity.is_subjective:
    89→        return pattern, load_matches(
    90→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    91→        )
    92→    matches = _load_dimension_issues(state, entity, status_filter)
    93→    if not matches:
    94→        _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    95→        return None
    96→    return entity.display_name, matches
    97→
    98→
    99→def _active_plan_or_none() -> dict | None:
   100→    try:
   101→        plan = load_plan()
   102→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   103→        return None
   104→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"):
   105→        return plan
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   110→    """Show all issues for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   111→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   112→    state = runtime.state
   113→    config = runtime.config
   114→
   115→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   119→    if skill_warning:
   120→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   121→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   122→    if config_warning:
   123→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   124→
   125→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   126→
   127→    opts = ShowOptions.from_args(args)
   128→
   129→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   130→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   131→        return
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   141→    if _handle_special_entity_views(
   142→        entity=entity,
   143→        state=state,
   144→        config=config,
   145→        lang_name=lang_name,
   146→        pattern_raw=opts.pattern_raw,
   147→    ):
   148→        return
   149→
   150→    match_result = _load_entity_matches(
   151→        state=state,
   152→        entity=entity,
   153→        pattern=pattern,
   154→        status_filter=status_filter,
   155→        scope=scope,
   156→        chronic=opts.chronic,
   157→    )
   158→    if match_result is None:
   159→        return
   160→    pattern, matches = match_result
   161→
   162→    if not matches:
   163→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   164→        return
   165→
   166→    (
   167→        surfaced_matches,
   168→        hidden_by_detector,
   169→        noise_budget,
   170→        global_noise_budget,
   171→        budget_warning,
   172→    ) = resolve_noise(
   173→        config,
   174→        matches,
   175→        no_budget=opts.no_budget,
   176→    )
   177→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   178→
   179→    payload = build_show_payload(
   180→        surfaced_matches,
   181→        pattern,
   182→        status_filter,
   183→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   184→            total_matches=len(matches),
   185→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   186→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   187→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   188→        ),
   189→    )
   190→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   191→
   192→    if opts.output_file:
   193→        if write_show_output_file(opts.output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   194→            return
   195→        raise SystemExit(1)
   196→
   197→    render_issues(
   198→        surfaced_matches,
   199→        pattern=pattern,
   200→        status_filter=status_filter,
   201→        show_code=opts.show_code,
   202→        top=opts.top,
   203→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   204→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   205→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   206→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   207→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   208→    )
   209→    plan_active = _active_plan_or_none()
   210→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   211→    show_subjective_followup(
   212→        state,
   213→        target_strict_score_from_config(config),
   214→    )
   215→
   216→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   217→
   218→
   219→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   220→

    33→    _print_next_command,

    36→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,

    46→)

    48→
    49→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    53→    if args.status != "fixed":
    54→        return True
    55→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    56→    if validate_note_length(note):
    57→        return True
    58→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    63→    *,
    64→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    65→    all_resolved: list[str],
    66→    attestation: str | None,
    67→) -> dict | None:
    68→    plan = None
    69→    try:
    70→        if not has_living_plan():
    71→            return None
    72→        plan = load_plan()
    73→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    74→        append_log_entry(
    75→            plan,
    76→            "resolve",
    77→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
    78→            actor="user",
    79→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
    80→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
    81→        )
    82→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
    83→        if args.status == "fixed":
    84→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
    85→        elif args.status == "open":
    86→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    87→        save_plan(plan)
    88→        if purged:
    89→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
    90→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    91→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
    92→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    93→    return plan
    94→
    95→
    96→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    97→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    98→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    99→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   100→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    state_file = state_path(args)
   104→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   105→
   106→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if args.status == "fixed":
   110→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   111→            state=state,
   112→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   113→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   117→        state,
   118→        args,
   119→        attestation=attestation,
   120→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   121→    )
   122→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   123→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   124→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   125→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   126→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   127→    }
   128→
   129→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    if not all_resolved:
   131→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   132→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   136→
   137→    plan = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   138→        args=args,
   139→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   140→        attestation=attestation,
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   144→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   145→        state,
   146→        status=args.status,
   147→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   148→    )
   149→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   150→    render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   151→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   152→        args=args,
   153→        state=state,
   154→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   155→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   159→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   160→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   161→        state,
   162→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   163→    )
   164→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   165→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   166→
   167→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   168→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   169→        ResolveQueryContext(
   170→            patterns=args.patterns,
   171→            status=args.status,
   172→            resolved=all_resolved,
   173→            next_command=next_command,
   174→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   175→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   176→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   177→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   178→            attestation=attestation,
   179→            narrative=narrative,
   180→            state=state,
   181→        )
   182→    )
   183→
   184→    if args.status == "fixed" and plan is not None and next_command == "desloppify next":
   185→        print_user_message(
   186→            "Hey — nice, on to the next one. Run `desloppify next`"
   187→            " to pick up your next task. If this doesn't seem like"
   188→            " the right thing to work on, run `desloppify plan show`"
   189→            " to see the full queue, or `desloppify plan --help`"
   190→            " for all plan tools. Please don't stop until you're"
   191→            " done — unless I've told you otherwise, just keep going."
   192→        )
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_suppress_pattern", "cmd_resolve"]
   196→

     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    12→
    13→from .batch.orchestrator import do_import_run, do_run_batches
    14→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    15→from .importing.cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    16→from .merge import do_merge
    17→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    18→from .prepare import do_prepare
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class ReviewOptions:
    23→    """All user-facing review command options extracted once from argparse."""
    24→
    25→    merge: bool = False
    26→    run_batches: bool = False
    27→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    28→    external_start: bool = False
    29→    external_submit: bool = False
    30→    import_file: str | None = None
    31→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    32→    session_id: str | None = None
    33→    allow_partial: bool = False
    34→    scan_after_import: bool = False
    35→    path: str = "."
    36→    dry_run: bool = False
    37→    manual_override: bool = False
    38→    attested_external: bool = False
    39→    attest: str | None = None
    40→
    41→    @classmethod
    42→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    43→        return cls(
    44→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    45→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    46→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    47→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    48→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    49→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    50→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    51→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    52→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    53→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    54→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    55→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
    56→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
    57→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
    58→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
    59→        )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    63→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    64→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    65→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    66→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    67→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    68→            continue
    69→        try:
    70→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    71→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    72→            _ = exc
    73→
    74→
    75→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    76→    if lang:
    77→        return
    78→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    82→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    83→    return [
    84→        opts.merge,
    85→        opts.run_batches,
    86→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    87→        opts.external_start,
    88→        opts.external_submit,
    89→        import_mode,
    90→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    91→    ]
    92→
    93→
    94→def _validate_mode_selection(
    95→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    96→    *,
    97→    mode_flags: list[bool],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   100→        raise CommandError(
   101→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   102→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   103→            exit_code=1,
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   107→        raise CommandError(
   108→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   109→            exit_code=2,
   110→        )
   111→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   112→        raise CommandError(
   113→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   114→            exit_code=2,
   115→        )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _run_review_mode(
   119→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   120→    *,
   121→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   122→    runtime,
   123→    state,
   124→    lang,
   125→    state_file,
   126→) -> None:
   127→    if opts.merge:
   128→        do_merge(args)
   129→        return
   130→    if opts.run_batches:
   131→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   132→        do_run_batches(
   133→            args,
   134→            state,
   135→            lang,
   136→            state_file,
   137→            config=runtime.config,
   138→        )
   139→        return
   140→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   141→        do_import_run(
   142→            opts.import_run_dir,
   143→            state,
   144→            lang,
   145→            state_file,
   146→            config=runtime.config,
   147→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   148→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   149→            scan_path=opts.path,
   150→        )
   151→        return
   152→    if opts.external_start:
   153→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   154→        do_external_start(
   155→            args,
   156→            state,
   157→            lang,
   158→            config=runtime.config,
   159→        )
   160→        return
   161→    if opts.external_submit:
   162→        do_external_submit(
   163→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   164→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   165→            state=state,
   166→            lang=lang,
   167→            state_file=state_file,
   168→            config=runtime.config,
   169→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   170→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   171→            scan_path=opts.path,
   172→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   173→        )
   174→        return
   175→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   176→        do_validate_import(
   177→            opts.validate_import_file,
   178→            lang,
   179→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   180→            manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   181→            attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   182→            manual_attest=opts.attest,
   183→        )
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    if opts.import_file:
   187→        do_import(
   188→            opts.import_file,
   189→            state,
   190→            lang,
   191→            state_file,
   192→            config=runtime.config,
   193→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   194→            manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   195→            attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   196→            manual_attest=opts.attest,
   197→        )
   198→        return
   199→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   200→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   201→
   202→
   203→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   204→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   205→    _enable_live_review_output()
   206→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   207→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   208→    state = runtime.state
   209→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   210→    _require_lang(lang)
   211→
   212→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   213→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   214→    _validate_mode_selection(
   215→        opts,
   216→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   217→    )
   218→    _run_review_mode(
   219→        args,
   220→        opts=opts,
   221→        runtime=runtime,
   222→        state=state,
   223→        lang=lang,
   224→        state_file=state_file,
   225→    )
   226→
```

1→"""autofix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._autofix_preview import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→
    11→from .apply_flow import (
    12→    _apply_and_report,
    13→    _detect,
    14→    _print_fix_summary,
    15→    _report_dry_run,
    16→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    17→)
    18→from .options import _load_fixer
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_autofix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    23→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    24→
    25→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    26→    path = Path(args.path)
    27→
    28→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    29→
    30→    if not dry_run:
    31→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    32→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    33→    if not entries:
    34→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    38→    results = raw.entries
    39→    skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    40→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    41→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    42→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    43→
    44→    if dry_run and results:
    45→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    46→
    47→    if not dry_run:
    48→        _apply_and_report(
    49→            args,
    50→            path,
    51→            fixer,
    52→            fixer_name,
    53→            entries,
    54→            results,
    55→            total_items,
    56→            lang,
    57→            skip_reasons,
    58→        )
    59→    else:
    60→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    61→    print()
    62→

1→"""move command: move a file or directory and update all import references."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify import languages as lang_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.move.apply import apply_file_move
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.move.directory import run_directory_move
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.move.language import (
    12→    load_lang_move_module,
    13→    resolve_lang_for_file_move,
    14→    resolve_move_verify_hint,
    15→    supported_ext_hint,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.app.commands.move.planning import (
    18→    compute_replacements,
    19→    resolve_dest,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.app.commands.move.reporting import print_file_move_plan
    22→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import (
    23→    rel,
    24→    resolve_path,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    27→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    28→
    29→
    30→def cmd_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    31→    """Move a file or directory and update all import references."""
    32→    source_rel = args.source
    33→    source_abs = resolve_path(source_rel)
    34→    source_path = Path(source_abs)
    35→
    36→    if source_path.is_dir():
    37→        return _cmd_move_dir(args, source_abs)
    38→
    39→    if not source_path.is_file():
    40→        raise CommandError(f"Source not found: {rel(source_abs)}")
    41→
    42→    dest_abs = resolve_dest(source_rel, args.dest, resolve_path)
    43→    if Path(dest_abs).exists():
    44→        raise CommandError(f"Destination already exists: {rel(dest_abs)}")
    45→
    46→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    47→
    48→    lang_name = resolve_lang_for_file_move(source_abs, args)
    49→    if not lang_name:
    50→        raise CommandError(
    51→            "Cannot detect language. Use --lang or ensure file has one of: "
    52→            f"{supported_ext_hint()}"
    53→        )
    54→
    55→    lang = lang_mod.get_lang(lang_name)
    56→    move_mod = load_lang_move_module(lang_name)
    57→
    58→    scan_path = Path(resolve_path(lang.default_src))
    59→    importer_changes, self_changes = compute_replacements(
    60→        move_mod,
    61→        source_abs,
    62→        dest_abs,
    63→        lang.build_dep_graph(scan_path),
    64→    )
    65→
    66→    print_file_move_plan(source_abs, dest_abs, importer_changes, self_changes)
    67→    if dry_run:
    68→        print(colorize("  Dry run — no files modified.", "yellow"))
    69→        return
    70→
    71→    apply_file_move(source_abs, dest_abs, importer_changes, self_changes)
    72→
    73→    print(colorize("  Done.", "green"))
    74→    verify_hint = resolve_move_verify_hint(move_mod)
    75→    if verify_hint:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Run `{verify_hint}` to verify.", "dim"))
    77→    print()
    78→
    79→
    80→def _cmd_move_dir(args, source_abs: str):
    81→    """Move a directory (package) and update all import references."""
    82→    run_directory_move(args, source_abs, resolve_path)
    83→

1→"""detect command: run a single detector directly (bypass state tracking)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import languages as lang_api
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang, resolve_lang_settings
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime_options import (
    12→    LangRuntimeOptionsError,
    13→    print_lang_runtime_options_error,
    14→    resolve_lang_runtime_options,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    17→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    18→from desloppify.languages import runtime as lang_runtime
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_detector_key(
    22→    detector: str, detect_commands: dict[str, object]
    23→) -> str | None:
    24→    """Resolve detector input to a command key."""
    25→    detector = detector.strip()
    26→    if detector in detect_commands:
    27→        return detector
    28→
    29→    normalized = detector.lower().replace("-", "_")
    30→    if normalized in detect_commands:
    31→        return normalized
    32→
    33→    denormalized = detector.lower().replace("_", "-")
    34→    if denormalized in detect_commands:
    35→        return denormalized
    36→
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def cmd_detect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    41→    """Run a single detector directly (bypass state tracking)."""
    42→    detector_input = args.detector
    43→
    44→    # Resolve language (from --lang flag or auto-detection)
    45→    lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
    46→
    47→    if not lang_cfg:
    48→        langs = ", ".join(lang_api.available_langs()) or "registered language plugins"
    49→        raise CommandError(
    50→            f"No language specified. Use --lang <name> (available: {langs})."
    51→        )
    52→
    53→    # Validate detector name
    54→    detector = _resolve_detector_key(detector_input, lang_cfg.detect_commands)
    55→    if detector is None:
    56→        available = ", ".join(sorted(lang_cfg.detect_commands))
    57→        raise CommandError(
    58→            f"Unknown detector for {lang_cfg.name}: {detector_input}\n"
    59→            f"  Available: {available}"
    60→        )
    61→
    62→    # Set default thresholds for detectors that expect them
    63→    if getattr(args, "threshold", None) is None:
    64→        if detector == "large":
    65→            args.threshold = lang_cfg.large_threshold
    66→        elif detector == "dupes":
    67→            args.threshold = 0.8
    68→
    69→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    70→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(runtime.config, lang_cfg)
    71→    try:
    72→        lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang_cfg)
    73→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
    74→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_cfg.name)
    75→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
    76→    lang = lang_runtime.make_lang_run(
    77→        lang_cfg,
    78→        overrides=lang_runtime.LangRunOverrides(
    79→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
    80→            runtime_options=lang_options,
    81→        ),
    82→    )
    83→    args.lang_runtime_options = dict(lang_options)
    84→    try:
    85→        lang.detect_commands[detector](args)
    86→    finally:
    87→        args.lang_runtime_options = None
    88→    if not getattr(args, "json", False):
    89→        scan_path = getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."
    90→        print(
    91→            colorize(f"\n  Next command: `desloppify scan --path {scan_path}`", "dim"),
    92→            file=sys.stderr,
    93→        )
    94→
```

1→"""zone command: show/set/clear zone classifications."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    12→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
    13→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap, Zone
    17→
    18→
    19→def cmd_zone(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    20→    """Handle zone subcommands: show, set, clear."""
    21→    action = getattr(args, "zone_action", None)
    22→    if action in (None, "show"):
    23→        _zone_show(args)
    24→    elif action == "set":
    25→        _zone_set(args)
    26→    elif action == "clear":
    27→        _zone_clear(args)
    28→    else:
    29→        raise CommandError("Usage: desloppify zone {show|set|clear}")
    30→
    31→
    32→def _zone_show(args):
    33→    """Show zone classifications for all scanned files."""
    34→    state_file = state_path(args)
    35→    if not state_file.exists():
    36→        raise CommandError("No state file found — run a scan first.")
    37→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    38→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    39→        raise CommandError("No language detected — run a scan first.")
    40→
    41→    path = Path(args.path)
    42→    overrides = command_runtime(args).config.get("zone_overrides", {})
    43→
    44→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    45→    zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    46→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=overrides or None
    47→    )
    48→
    49→    # Group files by zone
    50→    by_zone: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    51→    for f in sorted(files, key=lambda f: rel(f)):
    52→        zone = zone_map.get(f)
    53→        by_zone.setdefault(zone.value, []).append(f)
    54→
    55→    total = len(files)
    56→    print(colorize(f"\nZone classifications ({total} files)\n", "bold"))
    57→
    58→    for zone_val in ["production", "test", "config", "generated", "script", "vendor"]:
    59→        zone_files = by_zone.get(zone_val, [])
    60→        if not zone_files:
    61→            continue
    62→        print(colorize(f"  {zone_val} ({len(zone_files)} files)", "bold"))
    63→        for f in zone_files:
    64→            rp = rel(f)
    65→            is_override = rp in overrides
    66→            suffix = colorize(" (override)", "cyan") if is_override else ""
    67→            print(f"    {rp}{suffix}")
    68→        print()
    69→
    70→    if overrides:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {len(overrides)} override(s) active", "dim"))
    72→    print(colorize("  Override: desloppify zone set <file> <zone>", "dim"))
    73→    print(colorize("  Clear:    desloppify zone clear <file>", "dim"))
    74→    print_agent_plan(
    75→        ["Fix misclassified files, then re-scan."],
    76→        next_command="desloppify scan",
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _zone_set(args):
    81→    """Set a zone override for a file."""
    82→    filepath = args.zone_path
    83→    zone_value = args.zone_value
    84→
    85→    # Validate zone value
    86→    valid_zones = {z.value for z in Zone}
    87→    if zone_value not in valid_zones:
    88→        raise CommandError(
    89→            f"Invalid zone: {zone_value}. Valid: {', '.join(sorted(valid_zones))}"
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    normalized = rel(filepath)
    93→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    94→    config.setdefault("zone_overrides", {})[normalized] = zone_value
    95→    try:
    96→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    97→    except OSError as e:
    98→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    99→    print(f"  Set {normalized} → {zone_value}")
   100→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.", "dim"))
   101→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify scan`", "dim"))
   102→
   103→
   104→def _zone_clear(args):
   105→    """Clear a zone override for a file."""
   106→    filepath = args.zone_path
   107→
   108→    normalized = rel(filepath)
   109→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   110→    overrides = config.get("zone_overrides", {})
   111→    if normalized in overrides:
   112→        del overrides[normalized]
   113→        try:
   114→            config_mod.save_config(config)
   115→        except OSError as e:
   116→            raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
   117→        print(f"  Cleared override for {normalized}")
   118→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify scan` to apply.", "dim"))
   119→        print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify scan`", "dim"))
   120→    else:
   121→        print(colorize(f"  No override found for {normalized}", "yellow"))
   122→

1→"""config command: show/set/unset project configuration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.base.config import (
     9→    CONFIG_SCHEMA,
    10→    save_config,
    11→    set_config_value,
    12→    unset_config_value,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→
    17→
    18→def cmd_config(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Handle config subcommands: show, set, unset."""
    20→    action = getattr(args, "config_action", None)
    21→    if action == "set":
    22→        _config_set(args)
    23→    elif action == "unset":
    24→        _config_unset(args)
    25→    else:
    26→        _config_show(args)
    27→
    28→
    29→def _config_show(args):
    30→    """Print all config keys with current values and descriptions."""
    31→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    32→
    33→    print(colorize("\n  Desloppify Configuration\n", "bold"))
    34→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
    35→        value = config.get(key, schema.default)
    36→        is_default = value == schema.default
    37→
    38→        # Format display value
    39→        if schema.type is int and key.endswith("_days") and value == 0:
    40→            display = "never (0)"
    41→        elif isinstance(value, list):
    42→            display = ", ".join(value) if value else "(empty)"
    43→        elif isinstance(value, dict):
    44→            display = f"{len(value)} entries" if value else "(empty)"
    45→        else:
    46→            display = str(value)
    47→
    48→        default_tag = colorize(" (default)", "dim") if is_default else ""
    49→        print(f"  {key:<25} {display}{default_tag}")
    50→        print(colorize(f"  {'':25} {schema.description}", "dim"))
    51→    print()
    52→
    53→
    54→def _config_set(args):
    55→    """Set a config key to a value."""
    56→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    57→    key = args.config_key
    58→    value = args.config_value
    59→
    60→    try:
    61→        set_config_value(config, key, value)
    62→    except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:
    63→        raise CommandError(str(e)) from e
    64→
    65→    try:
    66→        save_config(config)
    67→    except OSError as e:
    68→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    69→    display = config[key]
    70→    if isinstance(display, int) and key.endswith("_days") and display == 0:
    71→        display = "never (0)"
    72→    print(colorize(f"  Set {key} = {display}", "green"))
    73→
    74→
    75→def _config_unset(args):
    76→    """Reset a config key to its default."""
    77→    config = command_runtime(args).config
    78→    key = args.config_key
    79→
    80→    try:
    81→        unset_config_value(config, key)
    82→    except KeyError as e:
    83→        raise CommandError(str(e)) from e
    84→
    85→    try:
    86→        save_config(config)
    87→    except OSError as e:
    88→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {e}") from e
    89→    default = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default
    90→    print(colorize(f"  Reset {key} to default ({default})", "green"))
    91→

1→"""langs command: list all available language plugins with depth and tools."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework import registry_state
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    11→from desloppify.languages._framework.discovery import load_all
    12→from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import SHARED_PHASE_LABELS
    13→from desloppify.languages._framework.resolution import make_lang_config
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→_DEPTH_BARS = {
    18→    "full": 8,
    19→    "standard": 5,
    20→    "shallow": 3,
    21→    "minimal": 2,
    22→}
    23→
    24→_DEPTH_MAX = 8
    25→
    26→
    27→def _depth_bar(depth: str) -> str:
    28→    """Render a depth bar like ████░░░░."""
    29→    filled = _DEPTH_BARS.get(depth, 2)
    30→    empty = _DEPTH_MAX - filled
    31→    return "\u2588" * filled + "\u2591" * empty
    32→
    33→
    34→def _get_tool_labels(cfg: LangConfig) -> str:
    35→    """Extract tool labels from phases."""
    36→    if cfg.integration_depth == "full":
    37→        return "custom detectors"
    38→    labels = [p.label for p in cfg.phases if p.label not in SHARED_PHASE_LABELS]
    39→    suffix = " (auto-fix)" if cfg.fixers else ""
    40→    return (", ".join(labels) if labels else "none") + suffix
    41→
    42→
    43→def cmd_langs(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """List all available languages with depth and tool info."""
    45→    load_all()
    46→
    47→    configs: list[tuple[str, LangConfig]] = []
    48→    for name, obj in sorted(registry_state.all_items()):
    49→        if isinstance(obj, LangConfig):
    50→            configs.append((name, obj))
    51→        else:
    52→            try:
    53→                cfg = make_lang_config(name, obj)
    54→                configs.append((name, cfg))
    55→            except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError, AttributeError) as exc:
    56→                logger.debug("Skipping unresolvable lang config %s: %s", name, exc)
    57→                continue
    58→
    59→    # Sort: full first, then standard, shallow, minimal; alphabetical within
    60→    depth_order = {"full": 0, "standard": 1, "shallow": 2, "minimal": 3}
    61→    configs.sort(key=lambda x: (depth_order.get(x[1].integration_depth, 9), x[0]))
    62→
    63→    # Column widths
    64→    name_width = max(len(name) for name, _ in configs) + 2 if configs else 14
    65→    name_width = max(name_width, 14)
    66→
    67→    print()
    68→    header = f"{'Language':<{name_width}}{'Depth':<14}Tools"
    69→    print(colorize(header, "bold"))
    70→    print("\u2500" * 60)
    71→
    72→    for name, cfg in configs:
    73→        depth = getattr(cfg, "integration_depth", "full")
    74→        bar = _depth_bar(depth)
    75→        tools = _get_tool_labels(cfg)
    76→        print(f"{name:<{name_width}}{bar}    {tools}")
    77→    print()
    78→

1→"""Developer utilities."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import ast
     7→import keyword
     8→import re
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.dev_scaffold_templates import build_scaffold_files
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    15→
    16→
    17→def cmd_dev(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    18→    """Dispatch developer subcommands."""
    19→    action = getattr(args, "dev_action", None)
    20→    if action == "scaffold-lang":
    21→        try:
    22→            _cmd_scaffold_lang(args)
    23→        except ValueError as ex:
    24→            raise CommandError(str(ex)) from ex
    25→        return
    26→    raise CommandError("Unknown dev action. Use `desloppify dev scaffold-lang`.")
    27→
    28→
    29→def _normalize_lang_name(raw: str) -> str:
    30→    name = raw.strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    31→    if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z][a-z0-9_]*", name):
    32→        raise ValueError("language name must match [a-z][a-z0-9_]*")
    33→    if keyword.iskeyword(name):
    34→        raise ValueError(f"language name cannot be a Python keyword: {name}")
    35→    return name
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_extensions(values: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    39→    out: list[str] = []
    40→    for val in values or []:
    41→        ext = val.strip()
    42→        if not ext:
    43→            continue
    44→        if not ext.startswith("."):
    45→            ext = "." + ext
    46→        if not re.fullmatch(r"\.[a-z0-9]+", ext):
    47→            raise ValueError(f"invalid extension: {val!r}")
    48→        out.append(ext)
    49→    if not out:
    50→        raise ValueError("at least one --extension is required")
    51→    seen: set[str] = set()
    52→    deduped: list[str] = []
    53→    for ext in out:
    54→        if ext not in seen:
    55→            seen.add(ext)
    56→            deduped.append(ext)
    57→    return deduped
    58→
    59→
    60→def _normalize_markers(values: list[str] | None) -> list[str]:
    61→    out: list[str] = []
    62→    for val in values or []:
    63→        marker = val.strip()
    64→        if marker and marker not in out:
    65→            out.append(marker)
    66→    return out
    67→
    68→
    69→def _class_name(lang_name: str) -> str:
    70→    return "".join(part.capitalize() for part in lang_name.split("_")) + "Config"
    71→
    72→
    73→def _template_files(
    74→    lang_name: str,
    75→    extensions: list[str],
    76→    markers: list[str],
    77→    default_src: str,
    78→) -> dict[str, str]:
    79→    class_name = _class_name(lang_name)
    80→    return build_scaffold_files(
    81→        lang_name=lang_name,
    82→        class_name=class_name,
    83→        extensions=extensions,
    84→        markers=markers,
    85→        default_src=default_src,
    86→    )
    87→
    88→
    89→def _render_array(items: list[str]) -> str:
    90→    return ", ".join(repr(x) for x in items)
    91→
    92→
    93→def _append_toml_array_item(text: str, key: str, value: str) -> str:
    94→    pattern = re.compile(
    95→        rf"(^\s*{re.escape(key)}\s*=\s*\[)(.*?)(\]\s*$)",
    96→        re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL,
    97→    )
    98→    match = pattern.search(text)
    99→    if not match:
   100→        return text
   101→
   102→    raw = match.group(2).strip()
   103→    parsed = ast.literal_eval("[" + raw + "]") if raw else []
   104→    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
   105→        return text
   106→    if value in parsed:
   107→        return text
   108→
   109→    parsed.append(value)
   110→    replacement = f"{match.group(1)}{_render_array(parsed)}{match.group(3)}"
   111→    return text[: match.start()] + replacement + text[match.end() :]
   112→
   113→
   114→def _wire_pyproject(lang_name: str) -> bool:
   115→    pyproject_path = get_project_root() / "pyproject.toml"
   116→    if not pyproject_path.is_file():
   117→        return False
   118→
   119→    original = pyproject_path.read_text()
   120→    updated = original
   121→    updated = _append_toml_array_item(
   122→        updated,
   123→        "testpaths",
   124→        f"desloppify/languages/{lang_name}/tests",
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    if updated != original:
   128→        safe_write_text(pyproject_path, updated)
   129→        return True
   130→    return False
   131→
   132→
   133→def _cmd_scaffold_lang(args) -> None:
   134→    raw_name = getattr(args, "name", "")
   135→    lang_name = _normalize_lang_name(raw_name)
   136→    extensions = _normalize_extensions(getattr(args, "extension", None))
   137→    markers = _normalize_markers(getattr(args, "marker", None))
   138→    default_src = getattr(args, "default_src", "src") or "src"
   139→    force = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   140→    wire_pyproject = bool(getattr(args, "wire_pyproject", True))
   141→
   142→    lang_dir = get_project_root() / "desloppify" / "languages" / lang_name
   143→    if lang_dir.exists() and not force:
   144→        raise CommandError(
   145→            f"Language directory already exists: {lang_dir}. Use --force to overwrite."
   146→        )
   147→
   148→    files = _template_files(lang_name, extensions, markers, default_src)
   149→    for rel_path, content in files.items():
   150→        target = lang_dir / rel_path
   151→        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   152→        if target.exists() and not force:
   153→            continue
   154→        safe_write_text(target, content)
   155→
   156→    wired = _wire_pyproject(lang_name) if wire_pyproject else False
   157→
   158→    print(colorize(f"Scaffolded language plugin: {lang_name}", "green"))
   159→    print(f"  Path: {lang_dir}")
   160→    print(f"  Extensions: {', '.join(extensions)}")
   161→    print(f"  Markers: {', '.join(markers) if markers else '(none)'}")
   162→    print(f"  pyproject.toml updated: {'yes' if wired else 'no'}")
   163→    print(
   164→        colorize(
   165→            "  Next: implement real phases/commands/detectors and run pytest.", "dim"
   166→        )
   167→    )
   168→
```
