```
1→"""exclude command: add path patterns and clean stale queue/state entries."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    13→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import matches_exclusion
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    15→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    17→    load_plan,
    18→    plan_path_for_state,
    19→    purge_ids,
    20→    save_plan,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def _state_file_for_runtime(runtime) -> Path:
    25→    """Resolve the effective state file path for a command runtime."""
    26→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    27→    if isinstance(state_file, Path):
    28→        return state_file
    29→    return state_mod.STATE_FILE
    30→
    31→
    32→def _prune_excluded_issues(state: dict, pattern: str) -> list[str]:
    33→    """Drop issues whose file path matches a new exclusion pattern."""
    34→    issues = state.get("issues")
    35→    if not isinstance(issues, dict):
    36→        return []
    37→
    38→    removed_ids = [
    39→        issue_id
    40→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
    41→        if isinstance(issue, dict)
    42→        and matches_exclusion(str(issue.get("file", "")), pattern)
    43→    ]
    44→    for issue_id in removed_ids:
    45→        issues.pop(issue_id, None)
    46→    return removed_ids
    47→
    48→
    49→def _purge_removed_ids_from_plan(state_file: Path, removed_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    50→    """Remove pruned issue IDs from queue/skips/clusters in plan.json."""
    51→    if not removed_ids:
    52→        return 0
    53→    plan_file = plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    54→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
    55→    purged = purge_ids(plan, removed_ids)
    56→    if purged:
    57→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
    58→    return purged
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_exclude(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Add a path pattern to the exclude list and clean cached queue/state."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    config = runtime.config
    65→    state = runtime.state
    66→    state_file = _state_file_for_runtime(runtime)
    67→
    68→    config_mod.add_exclude_pattern(config, args.pattern)
    69→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
    70→    try:
    71→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    72→    except OSError as exc:
    73→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {exc}") from exc
    74→
    75→    removed_ids: list[str] = []
    76→    plan_purged = 0
    77→    if state_file.exists():
    78→        removed_ids = _prune_excluded_issues(state, args.pattern)
    79→        if removed_ids:
    80→            try:
    81→                state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    82→            except OSError as exc:
    83→                raise CommandError(f"could not save state: {exc}") from exc
    84→
    85→            try:
    86→                plan_purged = _purge_removed_ids_from_plan(state_file, removed_ids)
    87→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    88→                print(
    89→                    colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"),
    90→                    file=sys.stderr,
    91→                )
    92→
    93→    print(colorize(f"Added exclude pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
    94→    if removed_ids:
    95→        print(f"  Removed {len(removed_ids)} matching issues from state.")
    96→        if plan_purged:
    97→            print(
    98→                colorize(
    99→                    f"  Plan updated: {plan_purged} item(s) removed from queue.",
   100→                    "dim",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   104→    if config_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   106→
   107→
   108→__all__ = ["cmd_exclude"]
   109→
```

```
1→"""update-skill command: install or update the desloppify skill document."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import urllib.error
     7→import urllib.request
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    12→from desloppify.app.skill_docs import (
    13→    SKILL_BEGIN,
    14→    SKILL_END,
    15→    SKILL_TARGETS,
    16→    SKILL_VERSION,
    17→    SKILL_VERSION_RE,
    18→    SkillInstall,
    19→    find_installed_skill,
    20→)
    21→
    22→_RAW_BASE = (
    23→    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/peteromallet/desloppify/main/docs"
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _download(filename: str) -> str:
    28→    """Download a file from the desloppify docs directory on GitHub."""
    29→    url = f"{_RAW_BASE}/{filename}"
    30→    with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=15) as resp:  # noqa: S310
    31→        return resp.read().decode("utf-8")
    32→
    33→
    34→def _build_section(skill_content: str, overlay_content: str | None) -> str:
    35→    """Assemble the complete skill section from downloaded parts."""
    36→    parts = [skill_content.rstrip()]
    37→    if overlay_content:
    38→        parts.append(overlay_content.rstrip())
    39→    return "\n\n".join(parts) + "\n"
    40→
    41→
    42→def _replace_section(file_content: str, new_section: str) -> str:
    43→    """Replace the desloppify section in a shared file, preserving surrounding content.
    44→
    45→    Uses first ``<!-- desloppify-begin -->`` and last ``<!-- desloppify-end -->``
    46→    so the overlay (which also has an end marker) is captured correctly.
    47→    """
    48→    begin = file_content.find(SKILL_BEGIN)
    49→    end = file_content.rfind(SKILL_END)
    50→    if begin == -1 or end == -1:
    51→        # No section markers — append (first install into existing shared file).
    52→        return file_content.rstrip() + "\n\n" + new_section
    53→
    54→    before = file_content[:begin]
    55→    after = file_content[end + len(SKILL_END):]
    56→    before = before.rstrip() + "\n\n" if before.strip() else ""
    57→    after = "\n" + after.lstrip("\n") if after.strip() else "\n"
    58→    return before + new_section + after
    59→
    60→
    61→def resolve_interface(
    62→    explicit: str | None = None,
    63→    install: SkillInstall | None = None,
    64→) -> str | None:
    65→    """Resolve which interface to update.
    66→
    67→    Uses the explicit argument if given, otherwise infers from an existing
    68→    install's overlay marker or file path.
    69→    """
    70→    if explicit:
    71→        return explicit.lower()
    72→
    73→    if install is None:
    74→        install = find_installed_skill()
    75→    if not install:
    76→        return None
    77→
    78→    if install.overlay:
    79→        return install.overlay.lower()
    80→
    81→    for name, (target, _overlay, _ded) in SKILL_TARGETS.items():
    82→        if target == install.rel_path:
    83→            return name
    84→    return None
    85→
    86→
    87→def update_installed_skill(interface: str) -> bool:
    88→    """Download and install the skill document for the given interface.
    89→
    90→    Returns True on success, False on failure. Prints status messages.
    91→    """
    92→    target_rel, overlay_name, dedicated = SKILL_TARGETS[interface]
    93→    target_path = get_project_root() / target_rel
    94→
    95→    print(colorize(f"Downloading skill document ({interface})...", "dim"))
    96→    try:
    97→        skill_content = _download("SKILL.md")
    98→        overlay_content = _download(f"{overlay_name}.md") if overlay_name else None
    99→    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError) as exc:
   100→        print(colorize(f"Download failed: {exc}", "red"))
   101→        return False
   102→
   103→    if "desloppify-skill-version" not in skill_content:
   104→        print(colorize("Downloaded content doesn't look like a skill document.", "red"))
   105→        return False
   106→
   107→    new_section = _build_section(skill_content, overlay_content)
   108→
   109→    target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   110→    if dedicated:
   111→        result = new_section
   112→    elif target_path.is_file():
   113→        existing = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   114→        result = _replace_section(existing, new_section)
   115→    else:
   116→        result = new_section
   117→
   118→    safe_write_text(target_path, result)
   119→
   120→    version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(new_section)
   121→    version = version_match.group(1) if version_match else "?"
   122→    print(
   123→        colorize(
   124→            f"Updated {target_rel} (v{version}, tool expects v{SKILL_VERSION})",
   125→            "green",
   126→        )
   127→    )
   128→    return True
   129→
   130→
   131→def cmd_update_skill(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   132→    """Install or update the desloppify skill document."""
   133→    interface = resolve_interface(getattr(args, "interface", None))
   134→
   135→    if not interface:
   136→        print(colorize("No installed skill document found.", "yellow"))
   137→        print()
   138→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   139→        print(f"Install with: desloppify update-skill <{names}>")
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    if interface not in SKILL_TARGETS:
   143→        names = ", ".join(sorted(SKILL_TARGETS))
   144→        print(colorize(f"Unknown interface '{interface}'.", "red"))
   145→        print(f"Available: {names}")
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    update_installed_skill(interface)
   149→
```

```
1→"""Suppress-pattern command handler extracted from resolve cmd module."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    12→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
    13→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    15→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    17→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    18→
    19→from .persist import _save_config_or_exit, _save_state_or_exit
    20→from .selection import show_attestation_requirement, validate_attestation
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_suppress_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Suppress issues matching a pattern."""
    25→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    26→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    27→        show_attestation_requirement("Suppress", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
    28→        raise CommandError("Suppress requires a valid attestation.")
    29→
    30→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    31→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    32→    state = runtime.state
    33→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    34→
    35→    config = runtime.config
    36→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
    37→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
    38→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
    39→
    40→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_issues(state, args.pattern)
    41→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
    42→        {
    43→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
    44→            "command": "suppress",
    45→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    46→            "attestation": attestation,
    47→            "affected": removed,
    48→        }
    49→    )
    50→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    51→
    52→    print(colorize(f"Added suppress pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
    53→    if removed:
    54→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching issues from state.")
    55→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    56→    if config_warning:
    57→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    58→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
    59→
    60→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    61→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    62→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    63→        state,
    64→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="suppress"),
    65→    )
    66→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    67→    write_query(
    68→        {
    69→            "command": "suppress",
    70→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    71→            "removed": removed,
    72→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    73→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    74→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    75→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    76→            "attestation": attestation,
    77→            "narrative": narrative,
    78→        }
    79→    )
    80→
    81→
    82→__all__ = ["cmd_suppress_pattern"]
    83→
```

```
1→"""CLI parser group builder for plan command family."""
     2→

     8→def add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
     9→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
    10→        "plan",
    11→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
    12→        description="""\
    13→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
    14→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
    15→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
    16→        epilog="""\
    17→typical workflow:
    18→  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
    19→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
    20→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
    21→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
    22→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
    23→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
    24→  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
    25→
    26→patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
    27→  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
    28→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
    29→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
    30→
    31→subcommands:
    32→  show       Show plan metadata summary
    33→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
    34→  reset      Reset plan to empty
    35→  reorder    Reposition issues or clusters in the queue
    36→  resolve    Mark issues as fixed (score movement + next-step)
    37→  describe   Set augmented description
    38→  note       Set note on issues
    39→  skip       Skip issues (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
    40→  unskip     Bring skipped issues back to queue
    41→  reopen     Reopen resolved issues
    42→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
    43→  cluster    Manage issue clusters
```

```
1→"""Autofix command option and fixer resolution helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import dataclasses
     6→from collections.abc import Callable
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixerConfig, LangConfig
    11→
    12→_COMMAND_POST_FIX: dict[str, Callable[..., None]] = {}
    13→
    14→
    15→def _load_fixer(args, fixer_name: str) -> tuple[LangConfig, FixerConfig]:
    16→    """Resolve fixer from language plugin registry, or exit."""
    17→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    18→    if not lang:
    19→        raise CommandError("Could not detect language. Use --lang to specify.")
    20→    if not lang.fixers:
    21→        raise CommandError(f"No auto-fixers available for {lang.name}.")
    22→    if fixer_name not in lang.fixers:
    23→        available = ", ".join(sorted(lang.fixers.keys()))
    24→        raise CommandError(
    25→            f"Unknown fixer: {fixer_name}\n  Available: {available}"
    26→        )
    27→    fc = lang.fixers[fixer_name]
    28→    if fixer_name in _COMMAND_POST_FIX and not fc.post_fix:
    29→        fc = dataclasses.replace(fc, post_fix=_COMMAND_POST_FIX[fixer_name])
    30→    return lang, fc
    31→
```

```
   172→def _resolve_fixer_results(
   173→    state: dict, results: list[dict], detector: str, fixer_name: str
   174→) -> list[str]:
   175→    resolved_ids = []
   176→    for r in results:
   177→        rfile = rel(r["file"])
   178→        for sym in r["removed"]:
   179→            fid = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{sym}"

   181→                state["issues"][fid]["status"] = "fixed"
   182→                state["issues"][fid]["note"] = (
   183→                    f"auto-fixed by desloppify autofix {fixer_name}"
   184→                )
   185→                resolved_ids.append(fid)
   186→    return resolved_ids

   189→def _warn_uncommitted_changes() -> None:
   190→    try:
   191→        r = subprocess.run(
   192→            ["git", "status", "--porcelain"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
   193→        )

   197→                    "\n  ⚠ You have uncommitted changes. Consider running:", "yellow"
   198→                )
   199→            )
   200→            print(
   201→                colorize(
   202→                    "    git add -A && git commit -m 'pre-fix checkpoint' && git push",
   203→                    "yellow",
   204→                )
   205→            )
   206→            print(
   207→                colorize(
   208→                    "    This ensures you can revert if the fixer produces unexpected results.\n",
   209→                    "dim",
   210→                )
   211→            )
   212→    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   213→        return
   214→
   215→
   216→def _cascade_unused_import_cleanup(
   217→    path: Path,
   218→    state: dict,
   219→    _prev_score: float,
   220→    dry_run: bool,
   221→    *,
   222→    lang: LangRun | None = None,
   223→) -> None:
   224→    if not lang or "unused-imports" not in getattr(lang, "fixers", {}):
   225→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no unused-imports fixer for this language", "dim"))
   226→        return
   227→
   228→    fixer = lang.fixers["unused-imports"]
   229→    print(colorize("\n  Running cascading import cleanup...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   230→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
   231→    if not entries:
   232→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
   236→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
   237→        results = raw.entries
   238→    else:
   239→        results = raw
   240→
   241→    if not results:
   242→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   243→        return
   244→    n_removed = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
   245→    n_lines = sum(r["lines_removed"] for r in results)
   246→    print(
   247→        colorize(
   248→            f"  Cascade: removed {n_removed} now-orphaned imports "
   249→            f"from {len(results)} files ({n_lines} lines)",
   250→            "green",
   251→        )
   252→    )
   253→    resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(
   254→        state, results, fixer.detector, "cascade-unused-imports"
   255→    )
   256→    if resolved:
   257→        print(f"  Cascade: auto-resolved {len(resolved)} import issues")
   258→
   259→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["debug-logs"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   260→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["dead-useeffect"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   261→
   262→_SKIP_REASON_LABELS = {
   263→    "rest_element": "has ...rest (removing changes rest contents)",
   264→    "array_destructuring": "array destructuring (positional — can't remove)",
   265→    "function_param": "function/callback parameter (use `fix unused-params` to prefix with _)",
   266→    "standalone_var_with_call": "standalone variable with function call (may have side effects)",
   267→    "no_destr_context": "destructuring member without context",
   268→    "out_of_range": "line out of range (stale data?)",
   269→    "other": "other patterns (needs manual review)",
   270→}
   271→
   272→
   273→def _print_fix_retro(
   274→    fixer_name: str,
   275→    detected: int,
   276→    fixed: int,
   277→    resolved: int,
   278→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   279→):
   280→    skipped = detected - fixed
   281→    print(colorize("\n  ── Post-fix check ──", "dim"))
   282→    print(
   283→        colorize(
   284→            f"  Fixed {fixed}/{detected} ({skipped} skipped, {resolved} issues resolved)",
   285→            "dim",
   286→        )
   287→    )
   288→    if skip_reasons and skipped > 0:
   289→        print(colorize(f"\n  Skip reasons ({skipped} total):", "dim"))
   290→        for reason, count in sorted(skip_reasons.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
   291→            print(
   292→                colorize(
   293→                    f"    {count:4d}  {_SKIP_REASON_LABELS.get(reason, reason)}", "dim"
   294→                )
   295→            )
   296→        print()
   297→    checklist = [
   298→        "Run your language typecheck/build command — does it still build?",
   299→        "Spot-check a few changed files — do the edits look correct?",
   300→    ]
   301→    if skipped > 0 and not skip_reasons:
   302→        checklist.append(
   303→            f"{skipped} items were skipped. Should the fixer handle more patterns?"
   304→        )
   305→    checklist += [
   306→        "Run `desloppify scan` to update state and refresh issues.",
   307→        "Are there cascading effects? (e.g., removing vars may orphan imports)",
   308→        "`git diff --stat` — review before committing. Anything surprising?",
   309→    ]
   310→    print(colorize("  Checklist:", "dim"))
   311→    for i, item in enumerate(checklist, 1):
   312→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. {item}", "dim"))
```

I now have a thorough understanding of the entire command structure. Here is my complete analysis.

---

## Full CLI Command Structure Analysis

### Overview

The CLI is built with argparse. The top-level parser is in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py`. Commands are registered in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/registry.py` which maps command name strings to handler functions. The parser groups are split across three files:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` -- scan, status, tree, show, next, suppress, exclude
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` -- detect, move, review, zone, config, autofix, viz, dev, langs, update-skill
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan.py` -- plan (with all subcommands)

The registry maps 18 top-level command names to handler functions.

---

### Command-by-Command Breakdown

#### 1. `scan`
- **Help text**: "Run all detectors, update state, show diff"
- **Handler**: `cmd_scan` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/cmd.py`
- **What it does**: Runs all language detectors, merges results into persistent state, shows a diff summary, dimension deltas, score changes, post-scan analysis/narrative, writes query.json, emits scorecard badge.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_scan`, `_print_scan_header`, `_print_scan_complete_banner`, `_show_scan_visibility`, `_show_coverage_preflight`, `_print_plan_workflow_nudge`
- **Naming notes**: Consistent. Clean.

#### 2. `status`
- **Help text**: "Score dashboard with per-tier progress"
- **Handler**: `cmd_status` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status/cmd.py`
- **What it does**: Loads state, computes scores/dimensions/narrative, shows a full dashboard with score summary, scan metrics, dimension table, tier progress, structural areas, review summary, agent plan, and writes query output.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_status`, `_print_score_section`, `_status_json_payload`
- **Naming notes**: Consistent. The help says "per-tier progress" but the code also shows dimension health, score model breakdown, structural areas, etc. -- it's really a full dashboard, not just "per-tier progress."

#### 3. `next`
- **Help text**: "Show next highest-priority open issue"
- **Handler**: `cmd_next` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/cmd.py`
- **What it does**: Builds a prioritized work queue, shows the top N items (default 1), supports filtering by scope/cluster/status, grouping, and multiple output formats.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_next`, `_get_items`, `NextOptions`, `_resolve_cluster_focus`, `_build_next_payload`, `_emit_requested_output`, `_plan_queue_context`, `_merge_potentials_safe`
- **Naming notes**: `_get_items` is a generic name for the core logic function that does queue building, rendering, and output. It could be more descriptive. Otherwise consistent.

#### 4. `plan`
- **Help text**: "Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note"
- **Handler**: `cmd_plan` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py`
- **What it does**: Dispatches to subcommands. With no subcommand, generates a full prioritized markdown plan. Subcommands: `show`, `queue`, `reset`, `reorder`, `describe`, `resolve`, `note`, `focus`, `skip`, `unskip`, `reopen`, `cluster` (with sub-sub-commands: create/add/remove/delete/reorder/show/list/merge/update), `triage`, `commit-log` (with sub-sub-commands: record/history/pr).
- **Key functions**: `cmd_plan` (dispatcher), `cmd_plan_output` / `_cmd_plan_generate` (markdown generation), `_cmd_plan_show`, `_cmd_plan_reset`
- **Naming notes**: 
  - `cmd_plan_output` is the public name, but `_cmd_plan_generate` is a private wrapper that just calls it. The indirection is odd -- `_cmd_plan_generate` could be removed.
  - The `_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS` dict is clean.
  - `plan resolve` is different from `resolve` -- `plan resolve` marks issues as fixed within the plan workflow (via `cmd_plan_resolve` in `override_handlers.py`), while `resolve` is not a top-level command at all.

#### 5. `show`
- **Help text**: "Dig into issues by file, directory, detector, or ID"
- **Handler**: `cmd_show` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`
- **What it does**: Shows issues filtered by pattern (file path, directory, detector name, issue ID, glob). Supports dimension views, concerns view, code snippets, chronic reopener filtering, noise budget.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_show`, `ShowOptions`, `_handle_special_entity_views`, `_load_entity_matches`, `_active_plan_or_none`
- **Naming notes**: Consistent.

#### 6. `suppress`
- **Help text**: "Suppress issues matching a pattern"
- **Handler**: `cmd_suppress_pattern` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/suppress.py`
- **What it does**: Adds a pattern to the ignore/suppress list in config, removes matching issues from state, requires attestation.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_suppress_pattern`
- **Naming notes**: The handler is named `cmd_suppress_pattern` but the command is just `suppress`. The handler lives inside the `resolve/` package, which is a bit surprising since `suppress` is its own top-level command, not a subcommand of resolve. The function name `cmd_suppress_pattern` includes "pattern" which is redundant with the command semantics (suppress always operates on patterns).

#### 7. `exclude`
- **Help text**: "Add path pattern to exclude list"
- **Handler**: `cmd_exclude` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/exclude.py`
- **What it does**: Adds a path pattern to the exclusion list in config, prunes matching issues from state and plan, marks config as needing rescan.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_exclude`, `_prune_excluded_issues`, `_purge_removed_ids_from_plan`, `_state_file_for_runtime`
- **Naming notes**: Consistent. Note the semantic overlap between `exclude` and `suppress` -- `exclude` removes path patterns from scanning entirely, `suppress` ignores specific issue patterns. The help text could be clearer on the distinction.

#### 8. `autofix`
- **Help text**: "Auto-fix mechanical issues"
- **Handler**: `cmd_autofix` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/autofix/cmd.py`
- **What it does**: Runs a named fixer (from the language plugin's fixer registry) to auto-fix mechanical issues. Detects issues, applies fixes (or dry-runs), auto-resolves matching issues in state, runs optional post-fix cascades.
- **Key functions**: `cmd_autofix`, `_load_fixer` (in `options.py`), `_detect`, `_print_fix_summary`, `_apply_and_report`, `_report_dry_run`, `_warn_uncommitted_changes`, `_resolve_fixer_results`, `_cascade_unused_import_cleanup`, `_print_fix_retro` (all in `apply_flow.py`)
- **Naming notes**: 
  - The internal variable in the parser builder is `p_fix` (line 381 of `parser_groups_admin.py`), not `p_autofix`. This is a minor inconsistency.
  - `_print_fix_retro` -- "retro" is jargon for retrospective/post-mortem. Could be clearer as `_print_fix_post_report` or similar.
  - The file `_autofix_preview.py` sits at the commands root level (not inside `autofix/`), exporting `show_fix_dry_run_samples`. This is a minor structural inconsistency.

### Naming Consistency Issues Identified

1. **`suppress` lives inside `resolve/` package**: The `cmd_suppress_pattern` handler is at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/suppress.py`, but `suppress` is a top-level command, not a subcommand of `resolve`. The `resolve/` package is otherwise about the `plan resolve` flow (resolve cmd.py, apply.py, persist.py, queue_guard.py, render.py, selection.py). `suppress` is conceptually separate from issue resolution.

2. **Handler name `cmd_suppress_pattern` vs command name `suppress`**: The handler has "pattern" in its name, which is redundant. Other handlers follow the pattern `cmd_{command_name}` -- `cmd_scan`, `cmd_status`, `cmd_next`, etc. This one is `cmd_suppress_pattern` rather than `cmd_suppress`.

3. **Parser variable `p_fix` for `autofix`**: In `parser_groups_admin.py` line 381, the parser variable is `p_fix` rather than `p_autofix`. Minor but inconsistent with how other parsers are named (`p_scan`, `p_status`, `p_show`, `p_next`, etc.).

4. **`_autofix_preview.py` at commands root**: The file `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/_autofix_preview.py` sits at the `commands/` root level rather than inside the `autofix/` subdirectory where the rest of the autofix code lives.

5. **`review` uses flag-based mode selection while similar commands use subcommands**: `zone`, `config`, `dev`, and `plan` all use argparse subcommands for their actions. `review` uses mutually exclusive boolean flags (`--merge`, `--run-batches`, `--prepare`, `--import`, `--external-start`, `--external-submit`). This creates an inconsistent user experience pattern.

6. **`_cmd_plan_generate` is a pointless wrapper**: In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py` lines 78-80, `_cmd_plan_generate` just calls `cmd_plan_output`. The indirection adds no value.

7. **`_get_items` in `next/cmd.py`**: The core logic function for the `next` command is named `_get_items` (line 209), which is extremely generic. Compare with `_cmd_plan_show`, `_zone_show`, `_config_set` -- all more descriptive.

8. **`resolve` is NOT a top-level command but has its own package**: The `resolve/` package at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/` exists and contains `cmd.py` with a `cmd_resolve` function. However, `resolve` is not registered as a top-level command in the registry -- it is only accessible as `plan resolve`. Despite this, the resolve package is imported by the plan override handlers. This means there is a full `cmd_resolve` function and `resolve/cmd.py` that looks like a standalone command but is actually called internally by `plan resolve`. The naming/structure suggests it was or was meant to be a top-level command.

9. **`status` help text undersells**: "Score dashboard with per-tier progress" -- but it actually shows dimension health, score model breakdown, structural areas, review summary, commit tracking, narrative/headline, focus suggestions, subjective followup. The help text is from an older era.

10. **`plan` help text inconsistency**: The parser help says "Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note" but the description and actual subcommand list also includes resolve, focus, unskip, reopen, triage, commit-log, show, queue, reset. The help text only names a subset of the subcommands.

```
   239→        return

   241→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact

   245→        issue_ids=issue_ids,

   286→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   287→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   288→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   289→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   290→    )
   291→
   292→
   293→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   294→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   295→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   296→    state = runtime.state
   297→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   301→
   302→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   303→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   304→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   305→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   306→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   307→    if not issue_ids:
   308→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   309→        return
   310→
   311→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, issue_ids)
   312→    append_log_entry(
   313→        plan, "unskip", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user",
   314→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   315→    )
   316→
   317→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   318→    reopened: list[str] = []
   319→    if need_reopen:
   320→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   321→        for fid in need_reopen:
   322→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   323→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   324→            plan=plan,
   325→            plan_path=plan_file,
   326→            state_data=state_data,
   327→            state_path_value=state_file,
   328→        )
   329→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   330→    else:
   331→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   332→
   333→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   334→
   335→
   336→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   337→# Reopen
   338→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   339→
   340→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   341→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   345→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   346→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   347→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   348→
   349→    reopened: list[str] = []
   350→    for pattern in patterns:
   351→        reopened.extend(
   352→            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open")
   353→        )
   354→
   355→    if not reopened:
   356→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   357→        return
   358→
   359→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   360→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   361→
   362→    # Remove from commit tracking uncommitted list
   363→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   364→
   365→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   366→    count = 0
   367→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   368→    for fid in reopened:
   369→        if fid in skipped:
   370→            skipped.pop(fid)
   371→            count += 1
   372→        if fid not in order:
   373→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   374→            order.add(fid)
   375→            count += 1
   376→    append_log_entry(
   377→        plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   378→    )
   379→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   380→        plan=plan,
   381→        plan_path=plan_file,
   382→        state_data=state_data,
   383→        state_path_value=state_file,
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   387→    if count:
   388→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   389→
   390→
   391→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   392→
   393→
   394→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   395→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   396→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   397→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   398→    for pattern in patterns:
   399→        if pattern in clusters:
   400→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   401→            # Filter to alive issues only — stale IDs should not count
   402→            ids = [
   403→                fid for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   404→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
   405→            ]
   406→            if len(ids) == 0:
   407→                print(colorize(
   408→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   409→                    "yellow",
   410→                ))
   411→                print(colorize(
   412→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>",
   413→                    "dim",
   414→                ))
   415→                return True  # blocked
   416→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   417→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   418→                return True  # blocked
   419→    return False  # OK
   420→
   421→
   422→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   423→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   424→    print(colorize(
   425→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   426→        "yellow",
   427→    ))
   428→    for fid in issue_ids:
   429→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   430→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   431→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   432→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   433→    print(colorize(
   434→        "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   435→        "dim",
   436→    ))
   437→    print(colorize(
   438→        "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   439→        "dim",
   440→    ))
   441→
   442→
   443→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   444→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real issue."""
   445→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   446→
   447→
   448→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   449→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.
   450→
   451→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   452→    """
   453→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   454→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   455→    return synthetic, remaining
   456→
   457→
   458→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   459→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   460→
   461→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   462→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   463→    """
   464→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   465→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   466→    if not present:
   467→        return {}
   468→
   469→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   470→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   471→
   472→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   473→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   474→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   475→            continue
   476→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   477→        unmet = sorted(
   478→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   479→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   480→        )
   481→        if unmet:
   482→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   483→    return blocked
   484→
   485→
   486→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   487→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   488→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   489→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   490→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   491→
   492→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   493→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   494→        if not note:
   495→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   496→            return
   497→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   498→        args.attest = attestation
   499→
   500→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   501→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   502→    if synthetic_ids:
   503→        plan = load_plan()
   504→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   505→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   506→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   507→            if sid in blocked_map:
   508→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   509→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   510→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   511→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   512→                    return
   513→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   514→        append_log_entry(
   515→            plan, "done", issue_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   516→        )
   517→        save_plan(plan)
   518→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   519→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   520→        if not real_patterns:
   521→            return
   522→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   523→        patterns = real_patterns
   524→        args.patterns = patterns
   525→
   526→    # Validate note length
   527→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   528→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   532→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   533→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan resolve", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   534→        return
   535→
   536→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   537→    try:
   538→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   539→        state = runtime.state
   540→        plan = load_plan()
   541→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   542→            return
   543→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   544→        plan = None
   545→
   546→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   547→    try:
   548→        if plan is None:
   549→            plan = load_plan()
   550→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   551→        cluster_name = None
   552→        for p in patterns:
   553→            if p in clusters:
   554→                cluster_name = p
   555→                break
   556→        append_log_entry(
   557→            plan,
   558→            "done",
   559→            issue_ids=patterns,
   560→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   561→            actor="user",
   562→            note=note,
   563→        )
   564→        save_plan(plan)
   565→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   566→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   567→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   568→
   569→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   570→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   571→        status="fixed",
   572→        patterns=patterns,
   573→        note=note,
   574→        attest=attestation,
   575→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   576→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   577→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   578→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   579→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   580→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   581→    )
   582→
   583→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   584→
   585→
   586→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   587→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   588→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   589→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   590→
   591→    plan = load_plan()
   592→    if clear_flag:
   593→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   594→        clear_focus(plan)
   595→        append_log_entry(
   596→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   597→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   598→        )
   599→        save_plan(plan)
   600→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   601→        return
   602→
   603→    if not cluster_name:
   604→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   605→        if active:
   606→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   607→        else:
   608→            print("  No active focus.")
   609→        return
   610→
   611→    try:
   612→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   613→    except ValueError as ex:
   614→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   615→        return
   616→    append_log_entry(
   617→        plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   618→        detail={"action": "set"},
   619→    )
   620→    save_plan(plan)
   621→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   622→
   623→
   624→__all__ = [
   625→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   626→    "cmd_plan_resolve",
   627→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   628→    "cmd_plan_note",
   629→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   630→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   631→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   632→]
   633→
```

```
    43→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    45→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    46→    state = runtime.state
    47→
    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)

    54→
    55→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)

    57→
    58→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    59→    if output:
    60→        try:
    61→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    62→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    63→            print_agent_plan(
    64→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    65→                next_command=next_command,
    66→            )
    67→        except OSError as e:
    68→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(plan_md)
    71→        print()
    72→        print_agent_plan(
    73→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],

    75→        )

    83→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    84→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    85→    plan = load_plan()
    86→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    87→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    89→    kind_counts = {
    90→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    91→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    92→    }
    93→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    94→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    95→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    97→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    98→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    99→
   100→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   101→
   102→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   103→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   104→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   105→    if total_skipped:
   106→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   107→    else:
   108→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   109→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   110→    if clusters:
   111→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   112→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   113→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   115→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   116→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   117→    if described or noted:
   118→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   119→    if active:
   120→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   121→    if superseded:
   122→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   123→
   124→    # Commit tracking summary
   125→    _cfg = load_config()
   126→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   127→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   128→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   129→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   130→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   131→            print(
   132→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   133→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   138→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   142→    reset_plan(plan)
   143→    append_log_entry(
   144→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   145→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   146→    )
   147→    save_plan(plan)
   148→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   149→
   150→
   151→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   152→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   153→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   154→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   155→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   156→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   157→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   158→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   159→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   160→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   161→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   162→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   163→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   164→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   165→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   166→}
   167→
   168→
   169→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   170→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   171→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   172→    if plan_action is None:
   173→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   177→    if handler is None:
   178→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   179→        return
   180→    handler(args)
   181→
   182→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
```

```
    15→def show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries: list[dict], results: list[dict]) -> None:
    16→    """Print sampled before/after context for fix --dry-run."""
    17→    random.seed(42)
    18→    print(colorize("\n  ── Sample changes (before → after) ──", "cyan"))
    19→    for result in random.sample(results, min(5, len(results))):
    20→        _print_fix_file_sample(result, entries)
    21→    removed_count = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    22→    if len(entries) > removed_count:
    23→        print(colorize(f"\n  Note: {len(entries) - removed_count} of {len(entries)} entries were skipped (complex patterns, rest elements, etc.)", "dim"))
    24→    print()
    25→
    26→
    27→def _print_fix_file_sample(result: dict, entries: list[dict]) -> None:
    28→    filepath, removed_set = result["file"], set(result["removed"])
    29→    try:
    30→        path = Path(filepath) if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else Path(".") / filepath
    31→        lines = path.read_text().splitlines()
    32→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    33→        _logger.debug("dry-run sample skipped for %s: %s", filepath, exc)
    34→        return
    35→
    36→    file_entries = [entry for entry in entries if entry["file"] == filepath and entry.get("name", "") in removed_set]
    37→    shown = 0
    38→    for entry in file_entries[:2]:
    39→        line_idx = entry.get("line", entry.get("detail", {}).get("line", 0)) - 1
    40→        if line_idx < 0 or line_idx >= len(lines):
    41→            continue
    42→        if shown == 0:
    43→            print(colorize(f"\n  {rel(filepath)}:", "cyan"))
    44→        name = entry.get("name", entry.get("summary", "?"))
    45→        ctx_s, ctx_e = max(0, line_idx - 1), min(len(lines), line_idx + 2)
    46→        print(colorize(f"    {name} (line {line_idx + 1}):", "dim"))
    47→        for idx in range(ctx_s, ctx_e):
    48→            marker = colorize("  →", "red") if idx == line_idx else "   "
    49→            print(f"    {marker} {idx+1:4d}  {lines[idx][:90]}")
    50→        shown += 1
    51→
```

```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    12→
    13→from .batch.orchestrator import do_import_run, do_run_batches
    14→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    15→from .importing.cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    16→from .merge import do_merge
    17→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    18→from .prepare import do_prepare
    19→
    20→
    21→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    22→class ReviewOptions:
    23→    """All user-facing review command options extracted once from argparse."""
    24→
    25→    merge: bool = False
    26→    run_batches: bool = False
    27→    import_run_dir: str | None = None
    28→    external_start: bool = False
    29→    external_submit: bool = False
    30→    import_file: str | None = None
    31→    validate_import_file: str | None = None
    32→    session_id: str | None = None
    33→    allow_partial: bool = False
    34→    scan_after_import: bool = False
    35→    path: str = "."
    36→    dry_run: bool = False
    37→    manual_override: bool = False
    38→    attested_external: bool = False
    39→    attest: str | None = None
    40→
    41→    @classmethod
    42→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ReviewOptions:
    43→        return cls(
    44→            merge=bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)),
    45→            run_batches=bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)),
    46→            import_run_dir=getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None),
    47→            external_start=bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)),
    48→            external_submit=bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)),
    49→            import_file=getattr(args, "import_file", None),
    50→            validate_import_file=getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None),
    51→            session_id=getattr(args, "session_id", None),
    52→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
    53→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
    54→            path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
    55→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
    56→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
    57→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
    58→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
    59→        )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    63→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    64→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    65→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    66→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    67→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    68→            continue
    69→        try:
    70→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    71→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    72→            _ = exc
    73→
    74→
    75→def _require_lang(lang) -> None:
    76→    if lang:
    77→        return
    78→    raise CommandError("Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", exit_code=1)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _mode_flags(opts: ReviewOptions) -> list[bool]:
    82→    import_mode = bool(opts.import_file) and not opts.external_submit
    83→    return [
    84→        opts.merge,
    85→        opts.run_batches,
    86→        bool(opts.import_run_dir),
    87→        opts.external_start,
    88→        opts.external_submit,
    89→        import_mode,
    90→        bool(opts.validate_import_file),
    91→    ]
    92→
    93→
    94→def _validate_mode_selection(
    95→    opts: ReviewOptions,
    96→    *,
    97→    mode_flags: list[bool],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
   100→        raise CommandError(
   101→            "Error: choose one review mode per command "
   102→            "(--merge | --run-batches | --import-run | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
   103→            exit_code=1,
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.import_file:
   107→        raise CommandError(
   108→            "Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
   109→            exit_code=2,
   110→        )
   111→    if opts.external_submit and not opts.session_id:
   112→        raise CommandError(
   113→            "Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
   114→            exit_code=2,
   115→        )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _run_review_mode(
   119→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   120→    *,
   121→    opts: ReviewOptions,
   122→    runtime,
   123→    state,
   124→    lang,
   125→    state_file,
   126→) -> None:
   127→    if opts.merge:
   128→        do_merge(args)
   129→        return
   130→    if opts.run_batches:
   131→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   132→        do_run_batches(
   133→            args,
   134→            state,
   135→            lang,
   136→            state_file,
   137→            config=runtime.config,
   138→        )
   139→        return
   140→    if opts.import_run_dir:
   141→        do_import_run(
   142→            opts.import_run_dir,
   143→            state,
   144→            lang,
   145→            state_file,
   146→            config=runtime.config,
   147→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   148→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   149→            scan_path=opts.path,
   150→        )
   151→        return
   152→    if opts.external_start:
   153→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   154→        do_external_start(
   155→            args,
   156→            state,
   157→            lang,
   158→            config=runtime.config,
   159→        )
   160→        return
   161→    if opts.external_submit:
   162→        do_external_submit(
   163→            import_file=str(opts.import_file),
   164→            session_id=str(opts.session_id),
   165→            state=state,
   166→            lang=lang,
   167→            state_file=state_file,
   168→            config=runtime.config,
   169→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   170→            scan_after_import=opts.scan_after_import,
   171→            scan_path=opts.path,
   172→            dry_run=opts.dry_run,
   173→        )
   174→        return
   175→    if opts.validate_import_file:
   176→        do_validate_import(
   177→            opts.validate_import_file,
   178→            lang,
   179→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   180→            manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   181→            attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   182→            manual_attest=opts.attest,
   183→        )
   184→        return
   185→
   186→    if opts.import_file:
   187→        do_import(
   188→            opts.import_file,
   189→            state,
   190→            lang,
   191→            state_file,
   192→            config=runtime.config,
   193→            allow_partial=opts.allow_partial,
   194→            manual_override=opts.manual_override,
   195→            attested_external=opts.attested_external,
   196→            manual_attest=opts.attest,
   197→        )
   198→        return
   199→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   200→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   201→
   202→
   203→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   204→    """Prepare or import subjective code review issues."""
   205→    _enable_live_review_output()
   206→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   207→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   208→    state = runtime.state
   209→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   210→    _require_lang(lang)
   211→
   212→    opts = ReviewOptions.from_args(args)
   213→    mode_flags = _mode_flags(opts)
   214→    _validate_mode_selection(
   215→        opts,
   216→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   217→    )
   218→    _run_review_mode(
   219→        args,
   220→        opts=opts,
   221→        runtime=runtime,
   222→        state=state,
   223→        lang=lang,
   224→        state_file=state_file,
   225→    )
   226→
```

```
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    28→

    30→from .persist import _save_state_or_exit
    31→from .queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
    32→from .render import (
    33→    _print_next_command,

    36→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    37→    render_commit_guidance,

    39→from .selection import (
    40→    ResolveQueryContext,

    42→    _previous_score_snapshot,
    43→    _validate_resolve_inputs,
    44→    show_note_length_requirement,
    45→    validate_note_length,
    46→)
    47→from .suppress import cmd_suppress_pattern
    48→
    49→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    53→    if args.status != "fixed":
    54→        return True
    55→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    56→    if validate_note_length(note):
    57→        return True
    58→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
    63→    *,
    64→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    65→    all_resolved: list[str],
    66→    attestation: str | None,
    67→) -> dict | None:
    68→    plan = None
    69→    try:
    70→        if not has_living_plan():
    71→            return None
    72→        plan = load_plan()
    73→        purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    74→        append_log_entry(
    75→            plan,
    76→            "resolve",
    77→            issue_ids=all_resolved,
    78→            actor="user",
    79→            note=getattr(args, "note", None),
    80→            detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
    81→        )
    82→        # Commit tracking: add to uncommitted on fix, remove on reopen
    83→        if args.status == "fixed":
    84→            add_uncommitted_issues(plan, all_resolved)
    85→        elif args.status == "open":
    86→            purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    87→        save_plan(plan)
    88→        if purged:
    89→            print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
    90→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    91→        _logger.debug("plan update failed after resolve", exc_info=True)
    92→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    93→    return plan
    94→
    95→
    96→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    97→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    98→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    99→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
   100→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    state_file = state_path(args)
   104→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   105→
   106→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if args.status == "fixed":
   110→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
   111→            state=state,
   112→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   113→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   117→        state,
   118→        args,
   119→        attestation=attestation,
   120→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
   121→    )
   122→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   123→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   124→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   125→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   126→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   127→    }
   128→
   129→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    if not all_resolved:
   131→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
   132→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} issues matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   136→
   137→    plan = _update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   138→        args=args,
   139→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   140→        attestation=attestation,
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   144→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   145→        state,
   146→        status=args.status,
   147→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   148→    )
   149→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   150→    render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   151→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   152→        args=args,
   153→        state=state,
   154→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   155→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   159→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   160→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   161→        state,
   162→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   163→    )
   164→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   165→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   166→
   167→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   168→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   169→        ResolveQueryContext(
   170→            patterns=args.patterns,
   171→            status=args.status,
   172→            resolved=all_resolved,
   173→            next_command=next_command,
   174→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   175→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   176→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   177→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   178→            attestation=attestation,
   179→            narrative=narrative,
   180→            state=state,
   181→        )
   182→    )
   183→
   184→    if args.status == "fixed" and plan is not None and next_command == "desloppify next":
   185→        print_user_message(
   186→            "Hey — nice, on to the next one. Run `desloppify next`"
   187→            " to pick up your next task. If this doesn't seem like"
   188→            " the right thing to work on, run `desloppify plan show`"
   189→            " to see the full queue, or `desloppify plan --help`"
   190→            " for all plan tools. Please don't stop until you're"
   191→            " done — unless I've told you otherwise, just keep going."
   192→        )
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_suppress_pattern", "cmd_resolve"]
   196→
```

```
1→"""Suppress-pattern command handler extracted from resolve cmd module."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    12→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
    13→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    14→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    15→from desloppify.base.tooling import check_config_staleness
    16→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    17→import desloppify.intelligence.narrative.core as narrative_mod
    18→
    19→from .persist import _save_config_or_exit, _save_state_or_exit
    20→from .selection import show_attestation_requirement, validate_attestation
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_suppress_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Suppress issues matching a pattern."""
    25→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    26→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    27→        show_attestation_requirement("Suppress", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
    28→        raise CommandError("Suppress requires a valid attestation.")
    29→
    30→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    31→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    32→    state = runtime.state
    33→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    34→
    35→    config = runtime.config
    36→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
    37→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
    38→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
    39→
    40→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_issues(state, args.pattern)
    41→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
    42→        {
    43→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
    44→            "command": "suppress",
    45→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    46→            "attestation": attestation,
    47→            "affected": removed,
    48→        }
    49→    )
    50→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    51→
    52→    print(colorize(f"Added suppress pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
    53→    if removed:
    54→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching issues from state.")
    55→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    56→    if config_warning:
    57→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    58→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
    59→
    60→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    61→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    62→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    63→        state,
    64→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="suppress"),
    65→    )
    66→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    67→    write_query(
    68→        {
    69→            "command": "suppress",
    70→            "pattern": args.pattern,
    71→            "removed": removed,
    72→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    73→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    74→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    75→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    76→            "attestation": attestation,
    77→            "narrative": narrative,
    78→        }
    79→    )
    80→
    81→
    82→__all__ = ["cmd_suppress_pattern"]
    83→
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import (
    10→    write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    14→
    15→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    21→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its issue IDs."""
    22→    try:
    23→        if not has_living_plan():
    24→            return None
    25→        plan = load_plan()
    26→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    27→        if cluster and cluster.get("issue_ids"):
    28→            return list(cluster["issue_ids"])
    29→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    30→        _logger.debug("cluster expansion skipped for %r", pattern, exc_info=True)
    31→    return None
    32→
    33→
    34→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    35→    state: dict,
    36→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    37→    *,
    38→    attestation: str | None,
    39→) -> list[str]:
    40→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    41→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    42→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    43→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    44→        if cluster_ids:
    45→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    46→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    47→                    state,
    48→                    fid,
    49→                    args.status,
    50→                    args.note,
    51→                    attestation=attestation,
    52→                )
    53→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    54→            continue
    55→
    56→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    57→            state,
    58→            pattern,
    59→            args.status,
    60→            args.note,
    61→            attestation=attestation,
    62→        )
    63→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    64→    return all_resolved
    65→
    66→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    67→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    68→    _write_query_best_effort(
    69→        {
    70→            "command": "resolve",
    71→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    72→            "status": context.status,
    73→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    74→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    75→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    76→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    77→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    78→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    79→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    80→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    81→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    82→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    83→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    84→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    85→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    86→        },
    87→        context="resolve query payload update",
    88→    )
    89→
```

```
1→"""Shared persistence helpers for resolve command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.base import config as config_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    10→
    11→
    12→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: Path | None) -> None:
    13→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    14→    try:
    15→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    16→    except OSError as exc:
    17→        raise CommandError(f"could not save state: {exc}") from exc
    18→
    19→
    20→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    21→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    22→    try:
    23→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    24→    except OSError as exc:
    25→        raise CommandError(f"could not save config: {exc}") from exc
    26→
    27→
    28→__all__ = ["_save_config_or_exit", "_save_state_or_exit"]
    29→
```
