```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_strict_target_nudge
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.render_support import (
    10→    print_post_resolve_guidance,
    11→    print_strict_gap_note,
    12→    score_snapshot_or_warn,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.base.config import load_config
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    16→from desloppify.base.git_context import detect_git_context
    17→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    18→from desloppify.engine.plan import get_uncommitted_issues, suggest_commit_message
    19→
    20→
    21→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    22→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    23→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} issue(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    24→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    25→        print(f"  {fid}")
    26→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    27→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    28→
    29→
    30→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    *,
    33→    status: str,
    34→    resolved_count: int,
    35→) -> None:
    36→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    37→        return
    38→    wontfix_count = sum(
    39→        1 for issue in state["issues"].values() if issue["status"] == "wontfix"
    40→    )
    41→    actionable = sum(
    42→        1
    43→        for issue in state["issues"].values()
    44→        if issue["status"]
    45→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    46→    )
    47→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} issues ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    51→            "yellow",
    52→        )
    53→    )
    54→    print(
    55→        colorize(
    56→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    57→            "dim",
    58→        )
    59→    )
    60→
    61→
    62→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    63→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    64→        return ""
    65→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    66→
    67→
    68→def _print_score_movement(
    69→    *,
    70→    status: str,
    71→    prev_overall: float | None,
    72→    prev_objective: float | None,
    73→    prev_strict: float | None,
    74→    prev_verified: float | None,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    has_review_issues: bool = False,
    77→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    78→) -> None:
    79→    new = score_snapshot_or_warn(state)
    80→    if new is None:
    81→        return
    82→
    83→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    84→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    85→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    86→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    87→    print(
    88→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    91→            "dim",
    92→        )
    93→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    94→        + colorize(
    95→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    96→        )
    97→    )
    98→    if target_strict is not None:
    99→        print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   100→    print_strict_gap_note(status, overall=new.overall, strict=new.strict)
   101→    print_post_resolve_guidance(
   102→        status=status,
   103→        has_review_issues=has_review_issues,
   104→        overall_delta=overall_delta,
   105→    )
   106→
   107→
   108→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   109→    *,
   110→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   111→    state: dict,
   112→    all_resolved: list[str],
   113→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   114→) -> None:
   115→    has_review = any(
   116→        state["issues"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   117→        for fid in all_resolved
   118→    )
   119→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    stale_dims = sorted(
   123→        dim
   124→        for dim in {
   125→            str(
   126→                state["issues"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   127→            ).strip()
   128→            for fid in all_resolved
   129→            if state["issues"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   130→        }
   131→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   132→    )
   133→    if not stale_dims:
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   137→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   138→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   139→    print(
   140→        colorize(
   141→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   142→            "yellow",
   143→        )
   144→    )
   145→    print(
   146→        colorize(
   147→            "  Next subjective step: "
   148→            + (
   149→                "`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   150→                f"{','.join(stale_dims)} --force-review-rerun`"
   151→            ),
   152→            "dim",
   153→        )
   154→    )
   155→
   156→
   157→def _render_uncommitted_block(uncommitted: list[str], just_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
   158→    """Print the uncommitted issues section."""
   159→    count = len(uncommitted)
   160→    just_set = set(just_resolved)
   161→    print(f"\n  Uncommitted work ({count} resolved issue{'s' if count != 1 else ''}):")
   162→    for fid in uncommitted[:5]:
   163→        marker = colorize("●", "green") if fid in just_set else "○"
   164→        tag = "  (just now)" if fid in just_set else ""
   165→        print(f"    {marker} {fid}{tag}")
   166→    if count > 5:
   167→        print(f"    ... and {count - 5} more")
   168→
   169→
   170→def _render_committed_block(commit_log: list[dict]) -> None:
   171→    """Print the already-committed section (last 3 commits)."""
   172→    if not commit_log:
   173→        return
   174→    committed_count = sum(len(r.get("issue_ids", [])) for r in commit_log)
   175→    nc = len(commit_log)
   176→    print(f"\n  Already committed ({nc} commit{'s' if nc != 1 else ''}, {committed_count} issue{'s' if committed_count != 1 else ''}):")
   177→    for record in commit_log[-3:]:
   178→        sha = record.get("sha", "?")[:7]
   179→        note = record.get("note", "")
   180→        fc = len(record.get("issue_ids", []))
   181→        note_str = f' — "{note}"' if note else ""
   182→        print(f"    {sha}{note_str} ({fc} issue{'s' if fc != 1 else ''})")
   183→
   184→
   185→def render_commit_guidance(
   186→    state: dict,
   187→    plan: dict | None,
   188→    just_resolved: list[str],
   189→    status: str,
   190→) -> None:
   191→    """Show commit tracking guidance after a resolve."""
   192→    if status != "fixed" or plan is None:
   193→        return
   194→
   195→    try:
   196→        config = load_config()
   197→        if not config.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   198→            return
   199→
   200→        git = detect_git_context()
   201→        if not git.available:
   202→            return
   203→
   204→        uncommitted = get_uncommitted_issues(plan)
   205→        if not uncommitted:
   206→            return
   207→
   208→        pr_number = config.get("commit_pr", 0)
   209→
   210→        print(colorize("\n  ── Commit Tracking ──────────────────────────", "dim"))
   211→        print(f"  Branch: {git.branch or '?'}    HEAD: {git.head_sha or '?'}")
   212→        if pr_number:
   213→            print(f"  PR: #{pr_number}")
   214→
   215→        _render_uncommitted_block(uncommitted, just_resolved)
   216→        _render_committed_block(plan.get("commit_log", []))
   217→
   218→        template = config.get(
   219→            "commit_message_template",
   220→            "desloppify: {status} {count} issue(s) — {summary}",
   221→        )
   222→        msg = suggest_commit_message(plan, template)
   223→        if msg:
   224→            print("\n  Suggested commit message:")
   225→            print(colorize(f'    "{msg}"', "cyan"))
   226→
   227→        print(colorize("\n  After committing → `desloppify plan commit-log record`", "dim"))
   228→        print(colorize("  ─────────────────────────────────────────────", "dim"))
   229→
   230→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   231→        logging.getLogger(__name__).debug(
   232→            "commit guidance rendering skipped", exc_info=True,
   233→        )
   234→
   235→
   236→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   237→    remaining = sum(
   238→        1
   239→        for issue in state["issues"].values()
   240→        if issue["status"] == "open"
   241→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   242→    )
   243→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   244→    if remaining > 0:
   245→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   246→        print(
   247→            colorize(
   248→                f"\n  {remaining} issue{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   249→                "dim",
   250→            )
   251→        )
   252→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   253→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   254→    print()
   255→    return next_command
   256→
```

```
1→"""Helper routines shared by resolve render output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def score_snapshot_or_warn(state: StateModel):
    11→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    12→    if (
    13→        snapshot.overall is None
    14→        or snapshot.objective is None
    15→        or snapshot.strict is None
    16→        or snapshot.verified is None
    17→    ):
    18→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    19→        return None
    20→    return snapshot
    21→
    22→
    23→def print_strict_gap_note(status: str, *, overall: float, strict: float) -> None:
    24→    if status != "wontfix":
    25→        return
    26→    strict_gap = round(overall - strict, 1)
    27→    if strict_gap <= 0:
    28→        return
    29→    print(
    30→        colorize(
    31→            f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    32→            f"Current gap: overall {overall:.1f} vs strict {strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
    33→            "yellow",
    34→        )
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def print_post_resolve_guidance(
    39→    *,
    40→    status: str,
    41→    has_review_issues: bool,
    42→    overall_delta: float,
    43→) -> None:
    44→    if has_review_issues and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
    45→        print(
    46→            colorize(
    47→                "  Scores unchanged (review issues don't affect scores directly).",
    48→                "yellow",
    49→            )
    50→        )
    51→        print(
    52→            colorize(
    53→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
    54→                "dim",
    55→            )
    56→        )
    57→        return
    58→    if status == "fixed":
    59→        print(
    60→            colorize(
    61→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the issue disappeared.",
    62→                "yellow",
    63→            )
    64→        )
    65→
    66→
    67→__all__ = [
    68→    "print_post_resolve_guidance",
    69→    "print_strict_gap_note",
    70→    "score_snapshot_or_warn",
    71→]
    72→
```

```
1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    14→
    15→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    16→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    17→_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50
    18→
    19→
    20→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    21→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    22→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class ResolveQueryContext:
    27→    patterns: list[str]
    28→    status: str
    29→    resolved: list[str]
    30→    next_command: str
    31→    prev_overall: float | None
    32→    prev_objective: float | None
    33→    prev_strict: float | None
    34→    prev_verified: float | None
    35→    attestation: str | None
    36→    narrative: dict
    37→    state: dict
    38→
    39→
    40→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    41→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    42→    return [
    43→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    44→    ]
    45→
    46→
    47→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    48→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    49→
    50→
    51→def show_attestation_requirement(
    52→    label: str,
    53→    attestation: str | None,
    54→    example: str,
    55→) -> None:
    56→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    57→    if not attestation:
    58→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    59→    elif missing:
    60→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    61→        _emit_warning(
    62→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    63→        )
    64→    _emit_warning(
    65→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    66→    )
    67→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    68→
    69→
    70→def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    71→    """Return True if the note meets the minimum length requirement."""
    72→    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
    73→
    74→
    75→def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
    76→    """Emit a warning about minimum note length."""
    77→    current = len((note or "").strip())
    78→    _emit_warning(
    79→        f"Note must be at least {_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH} characters (got {current}). "
    80→        f"Describe what you actually did."
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    85→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    86→        raise CommandError(
    87→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    88→        )
    89→    if args.status == "open":
    90→        return
    91→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    92→        show_attestation_requirement(
    93→            "Manual resolve",
    94→            attestation,
    95→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    96→        )
    97→        raise CommandError("Manual resolve requires a valid attestation.")
    98→
    99→
   100→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   101→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   102→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   106→    """Count unique open issues matching the provided patterns."""
   107→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   108→    for pattern in patterns:
   109→        for issue in state_mod.match_issues(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   110→            issue_id = issue.get("id")
   111→            if issue_id:
   112→                matched_ids.add(issue_id)
   113→    return len(matched_ids)
   114→
   115→
   116→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   117→    state: dict,
   118→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   119→    *,
   120→    attestation: str | None,
   121→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   122→) -> float:
   123→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   124→    before = state_mod.score_snapshot(state).strict
   125→    if before is None:
   126→        return 0.0
   127→
   128→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   129→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    after = state_mod.score_snapshot(preview_state).strict
   131→    if after is None:
   132→        return 0.0
   133→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   137→    state: dict,
   138→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   139→    *,
   140→    attestation: str | None,
   141→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   147→    if preview_count <= 10:
   148→        return
   149→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   153→        state,
   154→        args,
   155→        attestation=attestation,
   156→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   157→    )
   158→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} issues).")
   159→    if strict_delta > 0:
   160→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   161→    raise CommandError(
   162→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   163→    )
   164→
```

```
1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import queue_context
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    16→
    17→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _front_item_ids(front_item: dict) -> tuple[str, set[str]]:
    21→    front_id = front_item["id"]
    22→    front_ids = {front_id}
    23→    if front_item.get("kind") != "cluster":
    24→        return front_id, front_ids
    25→    for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    26→        front_ids.add(member["id"])
    27→    return front_id, front_ids
    28→
    29→
    30→def _resolve_target_ids(patterns: list[str], clusters: dict) -> set[str]:
    31→    resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    32→    for pattern in patterns:
    33→        if pattern in clusters:
    34→            resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("issue_ids", []))
    35→            resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    36→            continue
    37→        resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    38→    return resolved_ids
    39→
    40→
    41→def _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
    42→    resolved_ids: set[str],
    43→    *,
    44→    clusters: dict,
    45→    issues: dict,
    46→) -> set[str]:
    47→    return {
    48→        issue_id
    49→        for issue_id in resolved_ids
    50→        if issue_id in clusters
    51→        or (issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open")
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def _prune_front_covered_clusters(
    56→    out_of_order: set[str],
    57→    *,
    58→    clusters: dict,
    59→    issues: dict,
    60→    front_ids: set[str],
    61→) -> None:
    62→    for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    63→        if cluster_id not in clusters:
    64→            continue
    65→        alive_members = {
    66→            issue_id
    67→            for issue_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if issue_id in issues and issues[issue_id].get("status") == "open"
    69→        }
    70→        if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    71→            out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    72→
    73→
    74→def _print_queue_order_violation(front_id: str, out_of_order: set[str]) -> None:
    75→    print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    76→    for issue_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    77→        print(f"    {issue_id}")
    78→    print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    79→    print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan reorder <pattern> top            # move to front", "dim"))
    81→    print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    82→    print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    83→
    84→
    85→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    86→    state: dict,
    87→    patterns: list[str],
    88→    status: str,
    89→) -> bool:
    90→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    91→    if status != "fixed":
    92→        return False
    93→    try:
    94→        if not has_living_plan():
    95→            return False
    96→        plan = load_plan()
    97→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    98→        if not queue_order:
    99→            return False
   100→
   101→        ctx = queue_context(state, plan=plan)
   102→        result = build_work_queue(
   103→            state,
   104→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   105→                count=None,
   106→                include_subjective=True,
   107→                context=ctx,
   108→            ),
   109→        )
   110→        items = result["items"]
   111→        if not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   112→            items = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   113→        if not items:
   114→            return False
   115→
   116→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   117→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   118→        resolved_ids = _resolve_target_ids(patterns, clusters)
   119→        resolved_ids = _filter_open_or_cluster_targets(
   120→            resolved_ids,
   121→            clusters=clusters,
   122→            issues=issues,
   123→        )
   124→        if not resolved_ids:
   125→            return False
   126→
   127→        # Collect IDs from the first N queue positions where N is the
   128→        # number of items being resolved.  This allows batch-resolving
   129→        # a contiguous prefix (e.g. the first 9 items in one command).
   130→        prefix_depth = max(len(resolved_ids), 1)
   131→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
   132→        front_id = items[0]["id"]
   133→        for item in items[:prefix_depth]:
   134→            _, item_ids = _front_item_ids(item)
   135→            front_ids.update(item_ids)
   136→
   137→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
   138→        _prune_front_covered_clusters(
   139→            out_of_order,
   140→            clusters=clusters,
   141→            issues=issues,
   142→            front_ids=front_ids,
   143→        )
   144→        if not out_of_order:
   145→            return False
   146→
   147→        _print_queue_order_violation(front_id, out_of_order)
   148→        return True
   149→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   150→        _logger.debug("queue order guard skipped", exc_info=True)
   151→        return False
   152→
   153→
   154→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
   155→
```

```
   340→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   341→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   342→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   343→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   344→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   345→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   346→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   347→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   348→    z_set.add_argument(
   349→        "zone_value",
   350→        type=str,
   351→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   352→    )
   353→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   354→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   355→
   356→
   357→def _add_config_parser(sub) -> None:
   358→    p_config = sub.add_parser("config", help="Show/set/unset project configuration")
   359→    config_sub = p_config.add_subparsers(dest="config_action")
   360→    config_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show all config values")
   361→    c_set = config_sub.add_parser("set", help="Set a config value")
   362→    c_set.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   363→    c_set.add_argument("config_value", type=str, help="Value to set")
   364→    c_unset = config_sub.add_parser("unset", help="Reset a config key to default")
   365→    c_unset.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   366→
   367→
   368→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   369→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   370→    for lang_name in langs:
   371→        try:
   372→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   373→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   374→            fixer_names = []
   375→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   376→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   377→    return fixer_help_lines
   378→
   379→
   380→def _add_autofix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   381→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   382→        "autofix",
   383→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   384→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   385→    )
   386→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   387→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   388→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   389→    p_fix.add_argument(
   390→        "--dry-run",
   391→        action="store_true",
   392→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   393→    )
   394→
   395→

   399→    p_viz.add_argument("--output", type=str, default=None, help="Output file path")
   400→    p_viz.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   401→
   402→

   405→    dev_sub = p_dev.add_subparsers(dest="dev_action", required=True)
   406→    d_scaffold = dev_sub.add_parser(

   416→    )
   417→    d_scaffold.add_argument(
```

```
     8→def add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
     9→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
    10→        "plan",
    11→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
    12→        description="""\
    13→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
    14→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-issue annotations.
    15→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
    16→        epilog="""\
    17→typical workflow:
    18→  desloppify scan                       # detect issues
    19→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
    20→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
    21→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related issues
    22→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
    23→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
    24→  desloppify plan resolve <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
    25→
    26→patterns (used by reorder, skip, resolve, describe, note, etc.):
    27→  Patterns match issues by detector, file, ID prefix, glob, or name.
    28→  Cluster names also work as patterns — they expand to all member IDs.
    29→  Examples: "security", "src/foo.py", "unused::*React*", "my-cluster"
    30→
    31→subcommands:
    32→  show       Show plan metadata summary
    33→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
    34→  reset      Reset plan to empty
    35→  reorder    Reposition issues or clusters in the queue
    36→  resolve    Mark issues as fixed (score movement + next-step)
    37→  describe   Set augmented description
    38→  note       Set note on issues
    39→  skip       Skip issues (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
    40→  unskip     Bring skipped issues back to queue
    41→  reopen     Reopen resolved issues
    42→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
    43→  cluster    Manage issue clusters
    44→  triage     Staged triage workflow (after review)""",
    45→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    46→    )
    47→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
    48→    p_plan.add_argument(

    50→    )
```

```
1→"""autofix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._autofix_preview import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→
    11→from .apply_flow import (
    12→    _apply_and_report,
    13→    _detect,
    14→    _print_fix_summary,
    15→    _report_dry_run,
    16→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    17→)
    18→from .options import _load_fixer
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_autofix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    23→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    24→
    25→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    26→    path = Path(args.path)
    27→
    28→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    29→
    30→    if not dry_run:
    31→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    32→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    33→    if not entries:
    34→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    38→    results = raw.entries
    39→    skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    40→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    41→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    42→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    43→
    44→    if dry_run and results:
    45→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    46→
    47→    if not dry_run:
    48→        _apply_and_report(
    49→            args,
    50→            path,
    51→            fixer,
    52→            fixer_name,
    53→            entries,
    54→            results,
    55→            total_items,
    56→            lang,
    57→            skip_reasons,
    58→        )
    59→    else:
    60→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    61→    print()
    62→
```

```
     2→

     5→import argparse

     8→
     9→CommandHandler = Callable[[argparse.Namespace], None]
    10→
    11→
    12→def _build_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    13→    """Import all command modules and build the handler dict on first access."""
    14→    from desloppify.app.commands.config import cmd_config
    15→    from desloppify.app.commands.detect import cmd_detect
    16→    from desloppify.app.commands.dev import cmd_dev
    17→    from desloppify.app.commands.exclude import cmd_exclude
    18→    from desloppify.app.commands.autofix.cmd import cmd_autofix

    21→    from desloppify.app.commands.next import cmd_next
    22→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd import cmd_plan
    23→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.suppress import cmd_suppress_pattern
    24→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.cmd import cmd_review
    25→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.cmd import cmd_scan
    26→    from desloppify.app.commands.show.cmd import cmd_show
    27→    from desloppify.app.commands.status import cmd_status
    28→    from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import cmd_update_skill
    29→    from desloppify.app.commands.viz import cmd_tree, cmd_viz
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.zone import cmd_zone
    31→
    32→    return {
    33→        "scan": cmd_scan,
    34→        "status": cmd_status,
    35→        "show": cmd_show,
    36→        "next": cmd_next,
    37→        "suppress": cmd_suppress_pattern,
    38→        "exclude": cmd_exclude,
    39→        "autofix": cmd_autofix,
    40→        "plan": cmd_plan,
    41→        "detect": cmd_detect,
    42→        "tree": cmd_tree,
    43→        "viz": cmd_viz,
    44→        "move": cmd_move,
    45→        "zone": cmd_zone,
    46→        "review": cmd_review,
    47→        "config": cmd_config,
    48→        "dev": cmd_dev,
    49→        "langs": cmd_langs,
    50→        "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
    51→    }
    52→
    53→
    54→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    55→def get_command_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    56→    """Return cached command handler dict, building on first access."""
    57→    return _build_handlers()
    58→
    59→
    60→__all__ = ["CommandHandler", "get_command_handlers"]
```

```
     4→

    27→)
    28→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser_groups_plan import add_plan_parser
    29→
    30→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    31→workflow:
    32→  scan       Run detectors, update state, show diff
    33→  status     Score dashboard with dimension health
    34→  next       Show next highest-priority item to work on
    35→  plan       Living plan: prioritize, cluster, resolve, skip, annotate
    36→
    37→investigate:
    38→  show       Dig into issues by file/dir/detector/ID
    39→  tree       Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    40→  viz        Interactive HTML treemap
    41→  detect     Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    42→
    43→improve:
    44→  autofix    Auto-fix mechanical issues
    45→  suppress   Suppress issues matching a pattern
    46→  exclude    Exclude path pattern from scanning
    47→  move       Move file/dir and update import references
    48→  review     Holistic subjective review (LLM-based)
    49→
    50→configure:
    51→  zone       Show/set zone classifications
    52→  config     Project configuration
    53→  langs      List language plugins
    54→  dev        Developer utilities
    55→  update-skill  Install/update agent skill document
    56→
    57→examples:
    58→  desloppify scan
    59→  desloppify scan --skip-slow --profile ci
    60→  desloppify plan                        # full prioritized markdown
    61→  desloppify plan queue                  # compact table of all items
    62→  desloppify next --count 10             # top 10 queue items
    63→  desloppify show src/components/Modal.tsx
    64→  desloppify plan resolve "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
    65→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
    66→  desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import
    67→"""
    68→
    69→
    70→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    71→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    72→
    73→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    74→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    75→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    76→
    77→
    78→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    79→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    80→    try:
    81→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    82→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    83→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    84→
    85→
    86→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    87→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    88→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    89→
    90→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    91→        prog="desloppify",
    92→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    93→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    94→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    95→    )
    96→    parser.add_argument(
    97→        "--lang",
    98→        type=str,
    99→        default=None,
   100→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   101→    )
   102→    parser.add_argument(
   103→        "--exclude",
   104→        action="append",
   105→        default=None,
   106→        metavar="PATTERN",
   107→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   108→    )
   109→    parser.add_argument(
   110→        "--version",
   111→        "-V",
   112→        action="version",
   113→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   114→    )
   115→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   116→        dest="command",
   117→        required=True,
   118→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   119→    )
   120→    # workflow
   121→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   124→    add_plan_parser(sub)
   125→    # investigate

   127→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   130→    # improve

   139→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   140→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   141→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   142→    return parser
   143→
```

## 2. `override_handlers.py` -- How `plan resolve` dispatches to `cmd_resolve`

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`

The key function is `cmd_plan_resolve(args)` (line 486). The flow:

1. Extracts `patterns`, `attestation`, `note` from args
2. **`--confirm` shortcut**: If `--confirm` flag is set and `--note` exists, auto-generates attestation text from the note
3. **Synthetic IDs**: Separates `triage::*`, `workflow::*`, `subjective::*` patterns. These are resolved directly in the plan via `purge_ids()` -- they never go to `cmd_resolve` because they are not real state issues
4. **Validates**: note length, attestation keywords, cluster guard (blocks small clusters from bulk resolution)
5. **Logs**: Appends a `"done"` log entry to the plan
6. **Delegates**: Builds a new `argparse.Namespace` with `status="fixed"` and calls `cmd_resolve(resolve_args)` (line 583)

The `resolve_args` Namespace translation (lines 570-581):
```python
resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
    status="fixed",
    patterns=patterns,
    note=note,
    attest=attestation,
    confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
    force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    state=getattr(args, "state", None),
    lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
    path=getattr(args, "path", None),
    exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
)
```

So `plan resolve` is essentially a "fixed"-only wrapper with extra plan-aware behavior (synthetic ID handling, cluster guards, `--confirm` shortcut) that delegates to the same `cmd_resolve` used by the core resolve flow.

**Other handlers in this file**: `cmd_plan_describe`, `cmd_plan_note`, `cmd_plan_skip`, `cmd_plan_unskip`, `cmd_plan_reopen`, `cmd_plan_focus`.

**Imports from resolve package**:
- `resolve.cmd` -> `cmd_resolve`
- `resolve.selection` -> `show_attestation_requirement`, `show_note_length_requirement`, `validate_attestation`, `validate_note_length`

---

## 9. `review/cmd.py` -- Flag-based mode pattern

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/cmd.py`

Uses a `ReviewOptions` frozen dataclass (line 21) with boolean/optional flags extracted from `args`. The pattern:

1. `ReviewOptions.from_args(args)` extracts all flags from the Namespace
2. `_mode_flags(opts)` returns a list of booleans for mutually exclusive modes
3. `_validate_mode_selection(opts, mode_flags)` ensures at most one mode is active
4. `_run_review_mode(args, opts, ...)` dispatches via if/elif chain on the flag booleans:
   - `opts.merge` -> `do_merge(args)`
   - `opts.run_batches` -> `do_run_batches(...)`
   - `opts.import_run_dir` -> `do_import_run(...)`
   - `opts.external_start` -> `do_external_start(...)`
   - `opts.external_submit` -> `do_external_submit(...)`
   - `opts.validate_import_file` -> `do_validate_import(...)`
   - `opts.import_file` -> `do_import(...)`
   - (default) -> `do_prepare(...)` (prepare mode)

This is the flag-based dispatch pattern -- a single command with mutually exclusive `--flag` arguments rather than subcommands.
